  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 将这些靶点与ZINC15世界批准药物库中的6405种药物进行分子对接,生成了包含44,835种药物-蛋白配对的对接评分数据集,从中识别出多种高评分化合物。 [10.1007/s10822-026-00937-9]。

  • 研究发现,在复制体组装过程中,E5 经历大规模构象变化,从而能够与聚合酶 F8 拇指和 A22 亚基相互作用。这些变化与解旋酶激活相关并增强引物酶活性。 [10.1038/s41586-026-10937-2]。

  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Molecular docking of these targets against 6405 drugs from the ZINC15 world-approved subset generated a docking score dataset of 44,835 drug-protein pairs, from which numerous high-scoring compounds were identified.
  • MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45.
  • The study found that during replisome assembly, E5 undergoes large-scale conformational changes that enable interaction with the polymerase F8 thumb and A22 subunit.
  • Surface plasmon resonance measurements revealed nanomolar equilibrium dissociation constants of BA345 for A35R homologs, corroborating its cross-reactive, broad-spectrum neutralizing activity against orthopoxviruses.

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 世界卫生组织(WHO)发起了一项全球倡议以改善对猴痘 疫苗的获取。这是一项官方声明,已被确认为公共卫生行动。 [news-59f27ef7692da5c0]。
  • 加拿大确认了一名来自非洲的旅行者中出现的首例猴痘 I型病毒病例。世界卫生组织(WHO)和非洲疾病预防控制中心(Africa CDC)启动了'联合大陆应对计划',以应对猴痘 暴发。 [news-abfb3600ecc9f432]。
  • 文章提供了关于猴痘(猴痘)的一般信息,包括其症状、传播、预防措施以及老年人的风险因素。这是一篇指导性文章,而非暴发报告。 [news-ea7199493c4295a0]。

  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • The World Health Organization (WHO) launched a global initiative to improve access to mpox vaccines. This is an official statement and is confirmed as a public health action. [news-59f27ef7692da5c0].
  • Canada confirmed its first case of mpox clade I virus in a traveller from Africa. The WHO and Africa CDC launched a 'Joint Continental Response Plan' to address the mpox outbreak. [news-abfb3600ecc9f432].
  • The article provides general information about mpox (monkeypox), including its symptoms, transmission, prevention, and risk factors for older adults. It is a guidance article, not an outbreak report.
  • A new variant of mpox, or monkeypox, is killing children in Africa. The first reported human case of mpox was in 1970, in a 9-month-old boy in the DRC.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Reviews in medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70196  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 5, e70196
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

猴痘 机载 传播:系统回顾。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.
作者： Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
摘要中文翻译自 2022 年 5 月以来,多个非流行地区均报告了 猴痘,并在不同的地理环境中同时发生了 暴发,引发了人们对更广泛的国际传播的担忧。然而,有关空气传播 传播 风险的证据仍然有限。我们进行了系统审查,以评估空气中 传播 的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒。检索了 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,包括环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和 队列研究 研究。数据提取包括研究特征(作者、年份、设计和背景)、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、随访、评估的呼吸道颗粒类型(飞沫和/或气溶胶)以及主要发现。在确定的 286 份记录中,有四项研究符合纳入标准。所有这些都是 2022 年至 2023 年间发表的环境研究。在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 到 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或飞沫中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。总体而言,现有证据有限,并不支持空气传播感染的明确风险。需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸途径的潜力。系统审查注册:PROSPERO:CRD42023391123。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 猴痘 空气传播的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒,因为 猴痘 已在多个非流行地区报告,并担心国际传播。
设计与对象
对猴痘的空气传播传播的风险进行了系统评价,包括2022年至2023年期间发表的环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和队列研究研究。
核心方法
该综述涉及搜索 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,提取有关研究特征、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、评估的呼吸颗粒物和主要发现的数据。
主要结果
在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 至 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或液滴中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,现有证据并不支持空气传播感染的明显风险,强调需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸道途径的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,目前的证据不足以证实猴痘在空气中传播,这对公共卫生措施以及制定有针对性的监测和预防策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
证据有限且基于环境研究,可能无法完全代表人类 传播 动态,导致结论的置信度评估适中。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.

Authors: Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
Original abstract (English)
Since May 2022, mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions, with concurrent outbreaks in diverse geographic settings, raising concerns about broader international spread. However, evidence regarding the risk of airborne transmission remains limited. We conducted a systematic review to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission and the presence of viable viruses in air samples. PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched for studies published in English, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies. Data extraction included study characteristics (authors, year, design, and setting), sample size, diagnostic methods, participant characteristics, follow-up, type of respiratory particles assessed (droplets and/or aerosols), and key findings. Of 286 records identified, four studies met the inclusion criteria. All were environmental studies published between 2022 and 2023. MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission. Overall, the available evidence is limited and does not support a clear risk of airborne infection. Further well-designed studies in human settings are needed to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes. SYSTEMATIC REVIEW REGISTRATION: PROSPERO: CRD42023391123.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission of mpox and the presence of viable viruses in air samples, as mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions with concerns about international spread.
Design and population
A systematic review was conducted to assess the risk of airborne transmission of mpox, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies published between 2022 and 2023.
Core methods
The review involved searching PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library for studies published in English, extracting data on study characteristics, sample size, diagnostic methods, participant features, respiratory particles assessed, and key findings.
Main results
MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the available evidence does not support a clear risk of airborne infection, highlighting the need for further well-designed studies in human settings to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that current evidence is insufficient to confirm airborne transmission of mpox, which has implications for public health measures and the development of targeted surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited and based on environmental studies, which may not fully represent human transmission dynamics, leading to a moderate confidence assessment in the conclusions.

通过结构引导筛选发现针对猴痘病毒的交叉保护性人源单克隆抗体

Cross-protective human antibodies against the Mpox virus discovered through structure-guided screening
作者： Dongdong Sun, Zi-Han Jia, Hongyu Han, Nan Zhang, Yi-Ying Guo, Ruitian Hou, Wen-Li Zhao, Haisheng Yu, Lu-Lu Wang, Yu-Hui Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘病毒(MPXV)对全球健康构成日益严重的威胁,这一点在世界卫生组织两次宣布公共卫生国际关注事件中得到强调,特别是在刚果民主共和国出现了一种新型Clade Ib毒株,该毒株在人与人之间表现出高度传播性之后。然而,针对MPXV感染的治疗选择仍然极其有限。为解决这一未满足的需求,我们建立了一个结合单细胞转录组学和基于深度学习的结构预测的综合平台,以发现有效的针对MPXV的人源单克隆抗体。通过整合计算预测与实验验证,我们鉴定了五种针对以下不同病毒形式的中和抗体:BA345、MA42和MA49,这些抗体靶向与细胞外包膜病毒相关的A35R糖蛋白;BAL31,该抗体结合细胞内成熟病毒(IMV)蛋白A29L;以及HB05,该抗体靶向IMV抗原H3L。重要的是,精英单克隆抗体BA345在体外和体内均对MPXV和天花病毒提供了有效的保护作用。结合计算机模拟结构预测和X射线晶体学分析,我们发现了一种在正痘病毒中高度保守的表位。表面等离子体共振测量显示BA345对A35R同源物的平衡解离常数为纳摩尔级,证实了其对正痘病毒的交叉反应、广谱中和活性。此外,本研究中开发的BA345/BAL31和MA49/BAL31抗体组合在MPXV感染的动物中提供了强大的治疗保护作用,显著降低了疾病严重程度和病毒载量。我们的研究结果不仅通过将深度学习驱动的结构预测与单细胞多组学整合,建立了抗体发现的实用范式,还为针对MPXV及其相关正痘病毒的下一代生物防御对策提供了信息。

为解决这一未满足的需求,我们建立了一个结合单细胞转录组学和基于深度学习的结构预测的综合平台,以发现有效的针对MPXV的人源单克隆抗体。
设计与对象
此外,本研究中开发的BA345/BAL31和MA49/BAL31抗体组合在MPXV感染的动物中提供了强大的治疗保护作用,显著降低了疾病严重程度和病毒载量。
核心方法
提供的证据未明确说明核心的实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
表面等离子体共振测量显示BA345对A35R同源物的平衡解离常数为纳摩尔级,证实了其对正痘病毒的交叉反应、广谱中和活性。
解释与创新
摘要 猴痘病毒(MPXV)对全球健康构成日益严重的威胁,这一点在世界卫生组织两次宣布公共卫生国际关注事件中得到强调,特别是在刚果民主共和国出现了一种新型Clade Ib毒株,该毒株在人与人之间表现出高度传播性之后。
科研与公卫意义
然而,针对MPXV感染的治疗选择仍然极其有限。
局限与证据强度
我们的研究结果不仅通过将深度学习驱动的结构预测与单细胞多组学整合,建立了抗体发现的实用范式,还为针对MPXV及其相关正痘病毒的下一代生物防御对策提供了信息。

Cross-protective human antibodies against the Mpox virus discovered through structure-guided screening

Authors: Dongdong Sun, Zi-Han Jia, Hongyu Han, Nan Zhang, Yi-Ying Guo, Ruitian Hou, Wen-Li Zhao, Haisheng Yu, Lu-Lu Wang, Yu-Hui Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mpox virus (MPXV) poses an increasing global health threat, as underscored by two World Health Organization declarations of Public Health Emergencies of International Concern, particularly after the emergence of a novel Clade Ib strain that exhibited high human-to-human transmissibility in the Democratic Republic of the Congo. However, the treatment options for MPXV infection remain extremely limited. To address this unmet need, we established an integrated platform combining single-cell transcriptomics and deep learning-based structural prediction to discover effective human monoclonal antibodies against MPXV. By integrating computational prediction with experimental validation, we identified five neutralizing antibodies targeting the following distinct viral forms: BA345, MA42, and MA49, which engage the extracellular enveloped virus-associated A35R glycoprotein; BAL31, which binds intracellular mature virus (IMV) protein A29L; and HB05, which targets IMV antigen H3L. Importantly, the elite monoclonal antibody BA345 conferred effective protection against MPXV and vaccinia virus both in vitro and in vivo. Combined in silico structure prediction and X-ray crystallography revealed a highly conserved epitope shared across orthopoxviruses. Surface plasmon resonance measurements revealed nanomolar equilibrium dissociation constants of BA345 for A35R homologs, corroborating its cross-reactive, broad-spectrum neutralizing activity against orthopoxviruses. Moreover, the BA345/BAL31 and MA49/BAL31 antibody cocktails developed in this study conferred robust therapeutic protection in MPXV-infected animals, substantially reducing disease severity and viral load. Our findings not only establish a practical paradigm for antibody discovery through the integration of deep learning-driven structure prediction with single-cell multiomics but also inform next-generation biodefense countermeasures against MPXV and related orthopoxviruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To address this unmet need, we established an integrated platform combining single-cell transcriptomics and deep learning-based structural prediction to discover effective human monoclonal antibodies against MPXV.
Design and population
Moreover, the BA345/BAL31 and MA49/BAL31 antibody cocktails developed in this study conferred robust therapeutic protection in MPXV-infected animals, substantially reducing disease severity and viral load.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Surface plasmon resonance measurements revealed nanomolar equilibrium dissociation constants of BA345 for A35R homologs, corroborating its cross-reactive, broad-spectrum neutralizing activity against orthopoxviruses.
Interpretation and novelty
Abstract Mpox virus (MPXV) poses an increasing global health threat, as underscored by two World Health Organization declarations of Public Health Emergencies of International Concern, particularly after the emergence of a novel Clade Ib strain that exhibited high human-to-human transmissibility in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Scientific and public-health significance
However, the treatment options for MPXV infection remain extremely limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
Our findings not only establish a practical paradigm for antibody discovery through the integration of deep learning-driven structure prediction with single-cell multiomics but also inform next-generation biodefense countermeasures against MPXV and related orthopoxviruses.

针对猴痘病毒聚合酶和表面蛋白的结构预测与药物筛选

Structure prediction and drug screening targeting monkeypox virus polymerase and surface proteins
作者： Tao Tao, Linchuan Jia, Yuhang Cheng, Yawen Zou, Yanqin Wen, Yi Chen, Haowen Chen, Hao Wei, Qiangzhen Yang, Yongyong Shi
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒(MPXV)在全球范围内的暴发传播凸显了开发有效抗病毒药物治疗药物的迫切需求。在此研究中,我们整合了基于人工智能的蛋白质结构预测、大规模虚拟筛选和实验验证,以识别针对MPXV的初步命中化合物。利用AlphaFold2,我们预测了七种关键MPXV蛋白的高精度结构,包括三种与聚合酶相关的蛋白和四种表面蛋白。将这些靶点与ZINC15世界批准药物库中的6405种药物进行分子对接,生成了包含44,835种药物-蛋白配对的对接评分数据集,从中识别出多种高评分化合物。聚焦于A35R蛋白,我们选择了26种化合物进行表面等离子体共振(SPR)实验验证。其中三种化合物,包括长春碱碱(cepharanthine)、 eltrombopag和西米普利韦(simeprevir),显示出与A35R相关的可测量结合信号,其平衡解离常数(KD)处于微摩尔范围内。我们采用分子动力学(MD)模拟和分子力学通用Born表面积方法进行稳定性分析和相对能量评估。值得注意的是,这三种化合物此前已被报道靶向其他MPXV蛋白,这进一步支持了它们的再利用潜力。本研究确立了AI驱动的结构预测作为识别初步结合化合物的有用工具。然而,这些化合物尚未显示出抗病毒药物活性,因此这三种命中化合物需要进一步优化和生物学评估。我们的整合方法为应对新兴病毒威胁的快速药物筛选提供了框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒(MPXV)在全球范围内的暴发传播凸显了开发有效抗病毒药物治疗药物的迫切需求。
设计与对象
在此研究中,我们整合了基于人工智能的蛋白质结构预测、大规模虚拟筛选和实验验证,以识别针对MPXV的初步命中化合物。
核心方法
利用AlphaFold2,我们预测了七种关键MPXV蛋白的高精度结构,包括三种与聚合酶相关的蛋白和四种表面蛋白。
主要结果
将这些靶点与ZINC15世界批准药物库中的6405种药物进行分子对接,生成了包含44,835种药物-蛋白配对的对接评分数据集,从中识别出多种高评分化合物。
解释与创新
聚焦于A35R蛋白,我们选择了26种化合物进行表面等离子体共振(SPR)实验验证。
科研与公卫意义
本研究确立了AI驱动的结构预测作为识别初步结合化合物的有用工具。
局限与证据强度
然而,这些化合物尚未显示出抗病毒药物活性,因此这三种命中化合物需要进一步优化和生物学评估。

Structure prediction and drug screening targeting monkeypox virus polymerase and surface proteins

Authors: Tao Tao, Linchuan Jia, Yuhang Cheng, Yawen Zou, Yanqin Wen, Yi Chen, Haowen Chen, Hao Wei, Qiangzhen Yang, Yongyong Shi
Original abstract (English)
The global outbreak and ongoing spread of the monkeypox virus (MPXV) have highlighted the urgent need for effective antiviral therapeutics. Here, we integrated artificial intelligence-based protein structure prediction, large-scale virtual screening, and experimental validation to identify preliminary hit compounds targeting MPXV. Using AlphaFold2, we predicted high-accuracy structures for seven essential MPXV proteins, including three polymerase-related and four surface proteins. Molecular docking of these targets against 6405 drugs from the ZINC15 world-approved subset generated a docking score dataset of 44,835 drug-protein pairs, from which numerous high-scoring compounds were identified. Focusing on A35R, we selected 26 compounds for experimental validation using surface plasmon resonance (SPR). Three compounds, including cepharanthine, eltrombopag, and simeprevir, exhibited measurable A35R‐associated binding signals with equilibrium dissociation constants (KD) in the micromolar range. Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations and molecular mechanics generalized Born surface area were employed for stability analysis and relative energetic assessment. Notably, all three have been previously reported to target other MPXV proteins, reinforcing their potential for repurposing. This work establishes AI‐driven structure prediction as a useful tool for identifying preliminary binding compounds. However, no antiviral activity has been demonstrated for these compounds; therefore, the three hit compounds warrant further optimization and biological evaluation. Our integrated approach provides a framework for rapid drug screening against emerging viral threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The global outbreak and ongoing spread of the monkeypox virus (MPXV) have highlighted the urgent need for effective antiviral therapeutics.
Design and population
Here, we integrated artificial intelligence-based protein structure prediction, large-scale virtual screening, and experimental validation to identify preliminary hit compounds targeting MPXV.
Core methods
Using AlphaFold2, we predicted high-accuracy structures for seven essential MPXV proteins, including three polymerase-related and four surface proteins.
Main results
Molecular docking of these targets against 6405 drugs from the ZINC15 world-approved subset generated a docking score dataset of 44,835 drug-protein pairs, from which numerous high-scoring compounds were identified.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
However, no antiviral activity has been demonstrated for these compounds; therefore, the three hit compounds warrant further optimization and biological evaluation.

未被察觉的入境,被察觉的传播:中国首例Clade Ib猴痘病毒聚集的流行病学洞察

Unnoticed Entry, Noticed Transmission: Epidemiological Insights into China's First Clade Ib Monkeypox Virus Cluster.
作者： Xin Ye, Peng He, Baiyun Yu, Conghua Bai, Jingmin Chen, Qin Liu, Mingming Jiang, Minjie Xiang, Weiwen Guo, Mingqiang Huang
摘要中文翻译背景:由Clade Ib猴痘病毒(MPXV)引起的暴发在中非和东非地区迅速传播,导致世界卫生组织宣布暴发为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件(PHEIC)。为此,中国实施了加强的监测措施;然而,一名看似无症状的入境旅行者,尽管多次筛查检测结果为阴性,却引发了中国首例记录的Clade Ib 猴痘 传播聚集。方法:我们进行了流行病学、临床、环境和基因组学调查,以描述传播的模式并指导公共卫生响应。对人类和环境样本进行了实时聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)、血清学检测和全基因组测序,以确认感染并评估病毒污染情况。结果:确定了四名流行病学关联的个体,包括1例输入病例、2例因与输入病例进行无保护的异性接触而确诊的第一代病例,以及1例与亲密接触(整夜共睡)相关的疑似第二代病例。临床评估和随访期间均未记录发热症状;2例病例出现皮疹,1例出现腹股沟淋巴结病。全基因组测序确认感染为Clade Ib MPXV。从一般(非性接触)接触者收集的158份咽拭子样本均呈阴性。在314份环境样本中,从病例住所收集的62份样本中有25份MPXV qPCR检测呈阳性,而从公共环境收集的252份样本均为阴性。结论:这些发现表明,即使多次入境筛查结果为阴性,Clade Ib 猴痘 传播仍可能由看似无症状的入境旅行者引发,突显了仅依赖症状或入境点筛查的局限性,并强调了需要加强基于风险的监测、诊断检测和针对性预防策略的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨Clade Ib猴痘病毒(MPXV)在中国的传播动态,因为由Clade Ib MPXV引起的暴发在中非和东非地区迅速传播,并导致世界卫生组织宣布暴发为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件(PHEIC)。
设计与对象
本研究于2023年至2024年间在中国进行,聚焦于与一名看似无症状且多次筛查检测结果为阴性的入境旅行者相关的猴痘病例聚集。确定了四名流行病学关联的个体,包括1例输入病例、2例第一代确诊病例以及1例疑似第二代病例。
核心方法
研究人员进行了流行病学、临床、环境和基因组学调查,包括对人类和环境样本进行实时聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)、血清学检测和全基因组测序,以确认感染并评估病毒污染情况。
主要结果
确定了四名流行病学关联的个体,包括1例输入病例、2例因与输入病例进行无保护的异性接触而确诊的第一代病例,以及1例与亲密接触相关的疑似第二代病例。从一般接触者收集的158份咽拭子样本均呈阴性,从病例住所收集的62份样本中有25份MPXV qPCR检测呈阳性,而从公共环境收集的252份样本均为阴性。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,即使多次入境筛查结果为阴性,Clade Ib 猴痘 传播仍可能由看似无症状的入境旅行者引发,突显了仅依赖症状或入境点筛查的局限性。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提供了关于Clade Ib MPXV的传播模式的关键见解,强调了需要加强基于风险的监测、诊断检测和针对性预防策略的重要性,以应对无症状传播的潜在风险。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于仅关注单一聚集事件,并依赖于自我报告的接触信息,这可能引入偏差。由于聚集事件的具体背景和缺乏更广泛的人群数据,证据强度中等。

Unnoticed Entry, Noticed Transmission: Epidemiological Insights into China's First Clade Ib Monkeypox Virus Cluster.

Authors: Xin Ye, Peng He, Baiyun Yu, Conghua Bai, Jingmin Chen, Qin Liu, Mingming Jiang, Minjie Xiang, Weiwen Guo, Mingqiang Huang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The mpox outbreak caused by Clade Ib monkeypox virus (MPXV) spread rapidly in Central and East Africa and led the World Health Organization to declare the outbreak a public health emergency of international concern (PHEIC). In response, China implemented enhanced surveillance measures; nevertheless, an ostensibly asymptomatic returning traveler with repeatedly negative screening tests initiated China's first documented Clade Ib mpox transmission cluster. METHODS: We conducted an epidemiological, clinical, environmental, and genomic investigation to characterize the transmission pattern and inform public health response. Real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR), serological testing, and whole-genome sequencing were performed on human and environmental samples to confirm infection and assess viral contamination. RESULTS: Four epidemiologically linked individuals were identified, including 1 imported index case, 2 first-generation confirmed cases resulting from unprotected heterosexual contact with the index case, and 1 suspected second-generation case associated with intimate contact (overnight cosleeping). No fever was documented during clinical assessment or follow-up; 2 cases presented with rash, and 1 had inguinal lymphadenopathy. Whole-genome sequencing confirmed infection with Clade Ib MPXV. All 158 throat swabs collected from general (nonsexual) contacts tested negative. Among 314 environmental samples, 25 of 62 samples collected from case residences were MPXV qPCR positive, whereas all 252 samples from public settings were negative. CONCLUSIONS: These findings demonstrate that Clade Ib mpox transmission can occur from an apparently asymptomatic returning traveler despite repeated negative entry screening, underscoring the limitations of symptom-based or entry-point screening alone and the need for enhanced, risk-based surveillance, diagnostic testing, and targeted prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the transmission dynamics of Clade Ib monkeypox virus (MPXV) in China, as the mpox outbreak caused by Clade Ib MPXV spread rapidly in Central and East Africa and led the World Health Organization to declare the outbreak a public health emergency of international concern (PHEIC).
Design and population
The study was conducted in China between 2023 and 2024, focusing on a cluster of mpox cases linked to an ostensibly asymptomatic returning traveler who had repeatedly negative screening tests upon entry. Four epidemiologically linked individuals were identified, including one imported index case, two first-generation confirmed cases, and one suspected second-generation case.
Core methods
The researchers performed an epidemiological, clinical, environmental, and genomic investigation, including real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR), serological testing, and whole-genome sequencing on human and environmental samples to confirm infection and assess viral contamination.
Main results
Four epidemiologically linked individuals were identified, including 1 imported index case, 2 first-generation confirmed cases resulting from unprotected heterosexual contact with the index case, and 1 suspected second-generation case associated with intimate contact. All 158 throat swabs collected from general contacts tested negative, and 25 of 62 samples collected from case residences were MPXV qPCR positive, while all 252 samples from public settings were negative.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that Clade Ib mpox transmission can occur from an apparently asymptomatic returning traveler despite repeated negative entry screening, highlighting the limitations of symptom-based or entry-point screening alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical insights into the transmission patterns of Clade Ib MPXV, emphasizing the need for enhanced, risk-based surveillance, diagnostic testing, and targeted prevention strategies to address the potential for asymptomatic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single cluster and the reliance on self-reported contact information, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the specific context of the cluster and the lack of broader population data.

人源二抗混合物可针对真正的 猴痘病毒 提供预防和治疗保护

A human-derived two-antibody cocktail confers prophylactic and therapeutic protection against authentic Mpox virus
作者： Birte Kalveram, Awadalkareem Adam, Jimmy Kapetshi, Meredith Weglarz, George (Giorgi) Babuadze, Tian Wang, Hugues Fausther-Bovendo, Hugues Fausther‐Bovendo
摘要中文翻译摘要 随着全球范围内持续存在的人与人之间的 传播,猴痘病毒 仍然是全球重大的健康负担。然而,目前还没有针对 猴痘 的许可疗法,临床管理仅限于支持性护理和疼痛管理。鉴于病毒复杂的生命周期,有效的 治疗 需要抑制成熟的细胞内病毒粒子 (MV) 和细胞外病毒粒子 (EV)。在这里,我们描述了使用基于流式细胞术的细胞分选从抗原特异性记忆 B 细胞中分离人单克隆抗体 (mAb)。我们还使用体外中和测定和小鼠攻击模型来表征针对 MV 和 EV 的 2-mAb 混合物的治疗潜力。几种开发的人类 2-mAb 混合物在体外中和了正宗的 猴痘。在 猴痘 攻击之前或之后 24 小时施用时,先导 2-mAb 混合物可抑制小鼠组织(睾丸除外)中的病毒载量。总体而言,我们的研究确定了几种具有控制 猴痘 疾病治疗潜力的人类 2-mAb 混合物。意义 猴痘病毒 已成为重大公共卫生威胁,目前在全球多个国家流行。现有的 猴痘 疫苗 仅提供部分保护,而主要的 抗病毒药物 特考韦瑞 已证明没有临床益处,这凸显了对新疗法的迫切需要。认识到动物源性抗体在治疗用途上的局限性,我们分离了人类抗体,并使用体外中和测定和小鼠攻击模型证明了两种抗体混合物的治疗潜力。重要的是,我们的先导混合物中的两种抗体预计会与其 猴痘 靶标上的保守表位结合。总体而言,本研究记录了针对 猴痘 具有预防和治疗潜力的人类双抗体混合物的开发,为临床干预提供了一条有前途的新途径。

设计与对象
认识到动物源性抗体在治疗用途上的局限性,我们分离了人类抗体,并使用体外中和测定和小鼠攻击模型证明了两种抗体混合物的治疗潜力。

我们还使用体外中和测定和小鼠攻击模型来表征针对 MV 和 EV 的 2-mAb 混合物的治疗潜力。
主要结果
现有的 猴痘 疫苗 仅提供部分保护,而主要的 抗病毒药物 特考韦瑞 已证明没有临床益处,这凸显了对新疗法的迫切需要。

重要的是,我们的先导混合物中的两种抗体预计会与其 猴痘 靶标上的保守表位结合。

局限与证据强度
总体而言,本研究记录了针对 猴痘 具有预防和治疗潜力的人类双抗体混合物的开发,为临床干预提供了一条有前途的新途径。

A human-derived two-antibody cocktail confers prophylactic and therapeutic protection against authentic Mpox virus

Authors: Birte Kalveram, Awadalkareem Adam, Jimmy Kapetshi, Meredith Weglarz, George (Giorgi) Babuadze, Tian Wang, Hugues Fausther-Bovendo, Hugues Fausther‐Bovendo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT With sustained human-to-human transmission worldwide, Mpox virus remains a significant global health burden. However, there are no licensed therapeutics against Mpox, with clinical management limited to supportive care and pain management. Given the virus complex life cycles, effective treatments require the inhibition of both mature intracellular virions (MV) and extracellular virions (EV). Here, we describe the isolation of human monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) from antigen specific memory B cell using flow cytometry-based cell sorting. We also characterize the therapeutic potential of 2-mAb cocktails targeting both MV and EV using an in vitro neutralization assay and a mouse challenge model. Several developed human 2-mAb cocktails neutralized authentic Mpox in vitro . When administered 24 hours before or after Mpox challenge, the lead 2-mAb cocktail inhibited viral loads in mouse tissues, with the exception of the testes. Overall, our study identifies several human 2-mAb cocktails with therapeutic potential for controlling Mpox disease. SIGNIFICANCE Mpox virus, which has emerged as a significant public health threat, is now endemic to several countries worldwide. Existing Mpox vaccines offer only partial protection, and the primary antiviral, tecovirimat, has demonstrated no clinical benefits, highlighting an urgent need for novel therapeutics. Recognizing the limitations of animal-derived antibodies for therapeutic use, we isolated human antibodies and demonstrated the therapeutic potential of two-antibody cocktails using an in vitro neutralization assay and a mouse challenge model. Importantly, the two antibodies within our lead cocktail were predicted to bind to conserved epitopes on their Mpox targets. Overall, this study documents the development of a human two-antibody cocktail with both prophylactic and therapeutic potential against Mpox, offering a promising new avenue for clinical intervention.

猴痘病毒复制体的结构和工作原理

Structure and operating principles of a monkeypox virus replisome
作者： Zishuo Yu, Pradeep Sathyanarayana, Joel M. J. Tan, Side Hu, Xiaoyi Fan, Angela Gao, Philip J. Kranzusch, Joseph J. Loparo, Jonathan Abraham
摘要中文翻译摘要 痘病毒是具有大 基因组 的双链 DNA 病毒。其中,世界卫生组织宣布,猴痘病毒(MPXV)是最近两起突发公共卫生事件的罪魁祸首 1。MPXV 聚合酶包含三个亚基:催化亚基 (F8) 和异二聚体持续合成因子(A22 和 E4)。病毒聚合酶必须与六聚体解旋酶引物酶 (E5) 协调活动才能启动病毒 基因组 2 的复制。尽管 MPXV E5(参考文献 3,4)和聚合酶 5-7 的单独结构是可用的,但它们如何组装成功能性复制体仍不清楚。单独而言,E5 处于自抑制构象,解旋酶活性非常弱 3,4,并且解旋酶激活机制尚不清楚。在这里,我们使用冷冻电子显微镜来确定 DNA 结合的 MPXV 复制体的结构,该复制体包含聚合酶全酶(F8、A22 和 E4)和 E5 解旋酶六聚体。我们发现,在复制体组装过程中,E5 经历了大规模的构象变化,使其两个引物酶结构域与聚合酶 F8 拇指和 A22 亚基相互作用。生化测定和单分子实验表明,这种 E5 构象变化与解旋酶激活相关,并增强引物酶活性。总而言之,这些发现确定了在一类重要病毒病原体的 DNA 复制过程中控制协调解旋酶和聚合酶活性的基本机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 在 DNA 复制过程中协调其解旋酶和聚合酶活性的机制,从而解决病毒复制体功能组织方面的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用冷冻电子显微镜确定了 DNA 结合的 MPXV 复制体的结构,其中包括聚合酶全酶(F8、A22 和 E4)和 E5 解旋酶六聚体。该研究是在实验室环境中进行的,没有提及特定人群或 宿主。
核心方法
研究人员采用冷冻电子显微镜来确定 DNA 结合的 MPXV 复制体的结构。生化测定和单分子实验也用于研究构象变化及其功能含义。
主要结果
研究发现,在复制体组装过程中,E5 经历大规模构象变化,从而能够与聚合酶 F8 拇指和 A22 亚基相互作用。这些变化与解旋酶激活相关并增强引物酶活性。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了 MPXV DNA 复制过程中控制解旋酶和聚合酶协调活动的基本机制,为病毒复制体的功能组织提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为制定针对性的 抗病毒药物 策略提供信息,并提高我们对病毒 DNA 复制的理解,这对于 猴痘 疾病的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于实验室环境中的结构和生化分析,证据基于单一方法(冷冻电子显微镜),该方法提供了对复制体结构和功能的高置信度评估。

Structure and operating principles of a monkeypox virus replisome

Authors: Zishuo Yu, Pradeep Sathyanarayana, Joel M. J. Tan, Side Hu, Xiaoyi Fan, Angela Gao, Philip J. Kranzusch, Joseph J. Loparo, Jonathan Abraham
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Poxviruses are double-stranded DNA viruses with large genomes. Among them, monkeypox virus (MPXV) has been responsible for two recent public health emergencies as declared by the World Health Organization 1 . The MPXV polymerase comprises three subunits—a catalytic subunit (F8) and a heterodimeric processivity factor (A22 and E4). The viral polymerase must coordinate activities with the hexameric helicase–primase (E5) to initiate replication of the viral genome 2 . Although structures of MPXV E5 (refs. 3,4 ) and the polymerase 5–7 in isolation are available, how they assemble into a functional replisome remains unclear. In isolation, E5 is in an autoinhibited conformation and has very weak helicase activity 3,4 , and the mechanism for helicase activation is unclear. Here we used cryo-electron microscopy to determine the structures of DNA-bound MPXV replisomes comprising the polymerase holoenzyme (F8, A22 and E4) and the E5 helicase hexamer. We show that, during replisome assembly, E5 undergoes large-scale conformational changes that allow two of its primase domains to interact with the polymerase F8 thumb and A22 subunit. Biochemical assays and single-molecule experiments reveal that this E5 conformational change is coupled to helicase activation and enhances primase activity. Taken together, these findings identify fundamental mechanisms governing coordinated helicase and polymerase activities during DNA replication for an important class of viral pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the mechanisms by which monkeypox virus (MPXV) coordinates its helicase and polymerase activities during DNA replication, addressing a knowledge gap in the functional organization of the viral replisome.
Design and population
The study used cryo-electron microscopy to determine the structures of DNA-bound MPXV replisomes, which include the polymerase holoenzyme (F8, A22 and E4) and the E5 helicase hexamer. The research was conducted in a laboratory setting with no specific population or host mentioned.
Core methods
The researchers employed cryo-electron microscopy to determine the structures of DNA-bound MPXV replisomes. Biochemical assays and single-molecule experiments were also used to investigate the conformational changes and their functional implications.
Main results
The study found that during replisome assembly, E5 undergoes large-scale conformational changes that enable interaction with the polymerase F8 thumb and A22 subunit. These changes are coupled to helicase activation and enhance primase activity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal fundamental mechanisms that govern the coordinated activities of helicase and polymerase during DNA replication for MPXV, providing novel insights into the functional organization of the viral replisome.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of targeted antiviral strategies and improve our understanding of viral DNA replication, which is critical for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of mpox disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to structural and biochemical analyses in a laboratory setting, and the evidence is based on a single method (cryo-electron microscopy), which provides a high confidence assessment of the replisome structure and function.

葡萄牙医院猴痘病毒感染的临床和流行病学特征

Clinical and epidemiological characterization of monkeypox virus infections in a Portuguese hospital
作者： Ana C. Carvalho, Carlos M. Nogueira, Luísa Graça, Raquel Azevedo, Teresa Pereira, A. C. de P. Carvalho
摘要中文翻译目的:猴痘病毒感染历来被描述为非洲流行的人畜共患疾病,其特征是全身性水疱脓疱性皮疹和突出的全身症状。2022 年 暴发 揭示了独特的临床模式,具有持续性 传播 和非典型表现。本研究旨在描述葡萄牙医院诊断的 猴痘 病例的临床特征。方法:我们对2022 年 5 月至 2023 年 10 月期间诊断为 猴痘 的患者。诊断 通过分子检测得到证实。数据从电子病历中提取。结果:确定了 19 名患者,主要是男性,中位年龄为 32 岁。所有病例均归因于性 猴痘,主要发生在男男性行为者中。皮肤受累是普遍的,通常有有限数量的病变局限于生殖器或肛周区域。全身症状一般较轻。超过一半出现并发症,最常见的是直肠炎,其次是细菌重复感染。两名患者需要住院治疗。肛门或肛周病变与直肠炎显着相关。性传播合并感染很常见。患者仅需要有症状的 治疗。结论:该系列强调了最近 猴痘 表型的转变,其特征是有限病变负担、肛门生殖器受累为主、全身表现轻微。并发症频繁,需要密切监测,建议进行性传播感染筛查。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒感染历来被描述为非洲流行的人畜共患疾病,其特征是全身性水疱脓疱性皮疹和明显的全身症状。2022 年的 暴发 揭示了一种独特的临床模式,具有持续的性 传播 和非典型表现。
设计与对象
这项研究对 2022 年 5 月至 2023 年 10 月期间诊断为 猴痘 的患者进行了回顾性分析。共确定了 19 名患者,主要是男性,中位年龄为 32 岁。所有病例均归因于性行为 传播,主要发生在男男性行为者中。
核心方法
诊断通过分子测试得到证实。数据是从电子病历中提取的。
主要结果
皮肤受累是普遍的,通常只有有限数量的病变局限于生殖器或肛周区域。全身症状一般较轻。超过一半的人出现并发症,最常见的是直肠炎,其次是细菌重复感染。两名患者需要住院治疗。
解释与创新
本系列强调了最近 猴痘 表型的转变,其特点是病变负担有限、肛门生殖器受累为主和轻度全身表现。
科研与公卫意义
并发症很常见,需要密切监测,建议进行性传播感染筛查。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Clinical and epidemiological characterization of monkeypox virus infections in a Portuguese hospital

Authors: Ana C. Carvalho, Carlos M. Nogueira, Luísa Graça, Raquel Azevedo, Teresa Pereira, A. C. de P. Carvalho
Original abstract (English)
Objective: Monkeypox virus infection was historically described as a zoonotic disease endemic to Africa, characterized by a generalized vesiculopustular rash and prominent systemic symptoms.The 2022 outbreak revealed a distinct clinical pattern, with sustained sexual transmission and atypical presentations.This study aimed to characterize the clinical features of mpox cases diagnosed at a Portuguese hospital.Methods: We conducted a retrospective analysis of patients diagnosed with mpox between May 2022 and October 2023.Diagnosis was confirmed by molecular testing.Data were extracted from electronic medical records.Results: Nineteen patients were identified, predominantly male, with a median age of 32 years.All cases were attributed to sexual transmission, mainly among men who have sex with men.Cutaneous involvement was universal, typically with a limited number of lesions localized to the genital or perianal region.Systemic symptoms were generally mild.Over half developed complications, most commonly proctitis, followed by bacterial superinfection.Two patients required hospitalization.Anal or perianal lesions were significantly associated with proctitis.Sexually transmitted coinfections were frequent.Patients required only symptomatic treatment.Conclusion: This series highlights a shift in mpox phenotype during the recent outbreak, characterized by limited lesion burden, predominant anogenital involvement, and mild systemic manifestations.Complications were frequent, requiring close monitoring, and STI screening is recommended.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox virus infection was historically described as a zoonotic disease endemic to Africa, characterized by a generalized vesiculopustular rash and prominent systemic symptoms. The 2022 outbreak revealed a distinct clinical pattern, with sustained sexual transmission and atypical presentations.
Design and population
This study conducted a retrospective analysis of patients diagnosed with mpox between May 2022 and October 2023. Nineteen patients were identified, predominantly male, with a median age of 32 years. All cases were attributed to sexual transmission, mainly among men who have sex with men.
Core methods
Diagnosis was confirmed by molecular testing. Data were extracted from electronic medical records.
Main results
Cutaneous involvement was universal, typically with a limited number of lesions localized to the genital or perianal region. Systemic symptoms were generally mild. Over half developed complications, most commonly proctitis, followed by bacterial superinfection. Two patients required hospitalization.
Interpretation and novelty
This series highlights a shift in mpox phenotype during the recent outbreak, characterized by limited lesion burden, predominant anogenital involvement, and mild systemic manifestations.
Scientific and public-health significance
Complications were frequent, requiring close monitoring, and STI screening is recommended.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study.

在法国专业停留期间,对来自乌干达的 Ib 支猴痘病毒进行快速 诊断、基因组 测序和培养分离

Rapid diagnosis, genome sequencing and culture isolation of a monkeypox virus of clade Ib from a Ugandan during a professional stay in France
作者： Aymery Stheme de Jubecourt, Florian Cardona, Gwilherm Penant, Olivier Ferraris, Bernard La Scola, Philippe Colson, Jean Christophe Lagier, Aymery Stheme de Jubécourt

基因组分类采用Nextclade。该病例凸显了 MPXV 分支 Ib 正在进行的国际传播。

Rapid diagnosis, genome sequencing and culture isolation of a monkeypox virus of clade Ib from a Ugandan during a professional stay in France

Authors: Aymery Stheme de Jubecourt, Florian Cardona, Gwilherm Penant, Olivier Ferraris, Bernard La Scola, Philippe Colson, Jean Christophe Lagier, Aymery Stheme de Jubécourt
Original abstract (English)
A new MPXV lineage, clade Ib, was first identified in September 2023 in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC). Here, we report the fourth imported case of Monkeypox virus (MPXV) clade Ib identified in France and the first publicly available French genome sequence and culture isolate. MPXV clade Ib infection was detected by qPCR and confirmed by a clade Ib-specific qPCR. Whole-genome sequencing using Oxford Nanopore technology and viral culture on Vero E6 cells were both performed in a BSL3 laboratory. Genome classification used Nextclade. Clinical presentation and outcome were without particularites or complications. This case highlights the ongoing international dissemination of MPXV clade Ib and underscores the importance of genomic surveillance and viral isolation for monitoring emerging MPXV lineages in non-endemic countries.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the emergence and spread of a new Monkeypox virus (MPXV) lineage, clade Ib, in non-endemic countries. A new MPXV lineage, clade Ib, was first identified in September 2023 in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC).
Design and population
This study reports the fourth imported case of MPXV clade Ib identified in France and the first publicly available French genome sequence and culture isolate of this clade. The case involved a Ugandan individual during a professional stay in France.
Core methods
MPXV clade Ib infection was detected by qPCR and confirmed by a clade Ib-specific qPCR. Whole-genome sequencing using Oxford Nanopore technology and viral culture on Vero E6 cells were both performed in a BSL3 laboratory.
Main results
The clinical presentation and outcome were without particularites or complications.
Interpretation and novelty
Genome classification used Nextclade. This case highlights the ongoing international dissemination of MPXV clade Ib.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case underscores the importance of genomic surveillance and viral isolation for monitoring emerging MPXV lineages in non-endemic countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations. The evidence strength is moderate as the study reports a single case and does not provide broader statistical analysis.

2022 年 暴发 期间临床认可之前泰国 HIV 感染者中 猴痘病毒 暴露的血清学证据

Serologic Evidence of Mpox Virus Exposure Among People Living with HIV in Thailand Predating Clinical Recognition during the 2022 Outbreak
作者： Edgie-Mark A, Paramate Promnarate, Michelle Imbach, Kristina Peachman, Camila Macedo Cincotta, Nittaya Phanuphak, Lydie Trautmann, Sandhya Vasan, Eric C Garges
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 猴痘(也称为猴痘)可导致免疫功能低下个体显着发病,最近在非流行国家发生的多个 暴发 就证明了这一点。我们评估了泰国高危患者 队列研究 中的 正痘病毒 IgG 血清流行率,以确定既往暴露情况和相关临床/行为特征。方法 根据风险标准(多个伴侣、性传播感染病史或吸毒情况),从急性 HIV 队列研究 中选择存档的 2022 份标本。使用 Meso Scale Discovery V-PLEX 正痘病毒 IgG 测定法对样品进行测试。使用 Mann-Whitney U 和 Fisher 精确检验对人口统计、临床和行为数据进行分析。结果 491 名参与者中,5.5% (27/491) 血清呈阳性。早在 2022 年 1 月就检测到了血清阳性,早于泰国首例临床病例(2022 年 7 月)和废水检测(2022 年 6 月)。只有 7.4% (2/27) 的血清阳性参与者体检结果异常。非法药物使用 (74.1%) 和满足多种行为风险标准与血清阳性显着相关(分别为 p < 0.001 和 p = 0.029)。CD4 计数、HIV 病毒抑制或并发性传播感染方面没有发现显着差异。结论 未被识别的、缺乏症状的 猴痘 传播 在官方检测前几个月就出现在泰国。这些发现强调了血清学 监测 在已确定的高风险 队列研究 中对于早期检测新出现的感染威胁的效用。

问题与背景

早在 2022 年 1 月就检测到了血清阳性,早于泰国首例临床病例(2022 年 7 月)和废水检测(2022 年 6 月)。

Serologic Evidence of Mpox Virus Exposure Among People Living with HIV in Thailand Predating Clinical Recognition during the 2022 Outbreak

Authors: Edgie-Mark A, Paramate Promnarate, Michelle Imbach, Kristina Peachman, Camila Macedo Cincotta, Nittaya Phanuphak, Lydie Trautmann, Sandhya Vasan, Eric C Garges
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Objectives Mpox (also known as Monkeypox ) can cause significant morbidity in immunocompromised individuals, as evidenced in multiple recent outbreaks in non-endemic countries. We assessed orthopoxvirus IgG seroprevalence in an at-risk Thai cohort to identify prior exposure and associated clinical/behavioral features. Methods Archived 2022 specimens from an acute HIV cohort were selected based on risk criteria (multiple partners, STI history, or drug use). Samples were tested using the Meso Scale Discovery V-PLEX Orthopoxvirus IgG assay. Demographic, clinical, and behavioral data were analyzed using Mann–Whitney U and Fisher’s exact tests. Results Of 491 participants, 5.5% (27/491) were seropositive. Seropositivity was detected as early as January 2022, predating Thailand’s first clinical case (July 2022) and wastewater detection (June 2022). Only 7.4% (2/27) of seropositive participants had abnormal physical exams. Illicit drug use (74.1%) and meeting multiple behavioral risk criteria were significantly associated with seropositivity ( p < 0.001 and p = 0.029, respectively). No significant differences were found regarding CD4 counts, HIV viral suppression, or concurrent STIs. Conclusions Unrecognized, paucisymptomatic mpox transmission occurred in Thailand months before official detection. These findings highlight the utility of serologic surveillance within established high-risk cohorts for the early detection of emerging infectious threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
We assessed orthopoxvirus IgG seroprevalence in an at-risk Thai cohort to identify prior exposure and associated clinical/behavioral features.
Design and population
Methods Archived 2022 specimens from an acute HIV cohort were selected based on risk criteria (multiple partners, STI history, or drug use).
Core methods
Samples were tested using the Meso Scale Discovery V-PLEX Orthopoxvirus IgG assay.
Main results
Seropositivity was detected as early as January 2022, predating Thailand’s first clinical case (July 2022) and wastewater detection (June 2022).
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions Unrecognized, paucisymptomatic mpox transmission occurred in Thailand months before official detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the utility of serologic surveillance within established high-risk cohorts for the early detection of emerging infectious threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
No significant differences were found regarding CD4 counts, HIV viral suppression, or concurrent STIs.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

具有媒体效应的 猴痘 动力学分数阶模型的全局渐近稳定性

Global Asymptotic Stability of a Fractional-Order Model for Mpox Dynamics with Media Effects
作者： Ibraheem M. Alsulami, Fahad Al Basir
摘要中文翻译针对 猴痘 传播 动力学导出了分数阶数学模型,该模型通过新的依赖于意识的 传播 函数明确地纳入了公众意识的影响。所提出的 发病率 函数模拟了随着意识水平提高疾病 发病率 的减少,从而捕获流行病进展和行为反应之间的动态相互作用。该模型使用 Caputo 分数导数纳入记忆效应,进一步解释了无症状感染、住院治疗、康复和意识 进化。建立了模型的基本数学属性,包括正性、有界性和解的存在性。使用下一代矩阵方法导出基本再生数 R0,并进行敏感性分析以确定对疾病 传播 影响最大的流行病学参数。局部和全局稳定性分析表明,只要 R0 1 且在 R0=1 时发生前向跨临界分岔,无病平衡就局部和全局渐近稳定。数值模拟验证了分析结果,并说明了持续的公众意识在减少疾病 传播 和减轻流行病 暴发 方面的重要作用。从所提出的模型获得的结果表明,将行为意识策略与常规公共卫生干预措施相结合可以显着改善 猴痘 的长期控制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一个 猴痘 传播 动力学数学模型,其中纳入了公众意识对疾病传播的影响。该模型旨在捕捉流行病进展与对 猴痘 的行为反应之间的动态相互作用。
设计与对象
开发了分数阶数学模型来模拟 猴痘 传播 动态,纳入无症状感染、住院、康复和意识 进化 等因素。该模型通过数值模拟进行了验证,以说明公众意识对疾病 传播 和 暴发 缓解的影响。
核心方法
该模型使用 Caputo 分数导数来解释 猴痘 传播 中的记忆效应,并采用下一代矩阵方法来导出基本再生数 R0。进行敏感性分析以确定对疾病 传播 影响最大的流行病学参数。
主要结果
局部和全局稳定性分析表明,只要 R0 < 1,无病平衡就局部和全局渐近稳定,并且在 R0 = 1 时发生前向跨临界分岔。数值模拟验证了这些分析结果,并显示了持续的公众意识在减少疾病 传播 方面的重要作用。
解释与创新
作者解释说,持续的公众意识对于减少 猴痘 传播 和减轻流行病 暴发 起着至关重要的作用。本研究引入了一种新颖的数学框架,该框架使用分数阶导数将公众意识整合到 猴痘 传播 动力学中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将行为意识策略与常规公共卫生干预措施相结合可以显着改善 猴痘 的长期控制。这对于制定有针对性的公共卫生政策和宣传活动具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于数学建模,不包括来自现实世界 猴痘 暴发 的经验数据。证据强度中等,因为结果基于理论分析和模拟,而不是直接观察或实验数据。

Global Asymptotic Stability of a Fractional-Order Model for Mpox Dynamics with Media Effects

Authors: Ibraheem M. Alsulami, Fahad Al Basir
Original abstract (English)
A fractional-order mathematical model is derived for mpox transmission dynamics that explicitly incorporates the influence of public awareness through a new awareness-dependent transmission function. The proposed incidence function models the reduction in disease transmission as the level of awareness increases, thereby capturing the dynamic interaction between epidemic progression and behavioral response. The model further accounts for asymptomatic infection, hospitalization, recovery, and awareness evolution using Caputo fractional derivatives to incorporate memory effects. Basic mathematical properties of the model, including positivity, boundedness, and existence of solutions, are established. The basic reproduction number, R0, is derived using the next-generation matrix approach, and a sensitivity analysis is performed to identify the epidemiological parameters that most strongly influence disease transmission. Local and global stability analyses demonstrate that the disease-free equilibrium is locally and globally asymptotically stable whenever R0 1 and a forward transcritical bifurcation occurs at R0=1. Numerical simulations validate the analytical findings and illustrate the significant role of sustained public awareness in reducing disease transmission and mitigating epidemic outbreaks. The results obtained from the proposed model suggest that combining behavioral awareness strategies with conventional public health interventions can substantially improve the long-term control of mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a mathematical model for mpox transmission dynamics that incorporates the influence of public awareness on disease spread. The model was designed to capture the dynamic interaction between epidemic progression and behavioral response to mpox.
Design and population
A fractional-order mathematical model was developed to simulate mpox transmission dynamics, incorporating factors such as asymptomatic infection, hospitalization, recovery, and awareness evolution. The model was validated through numerical simulations to illustrate the impact of public awareness on disease transmission and outbreak mitigation.
Core methods
The model uses Caputo fractional derivatives to account for memory effects in mpox transmission, and a next-generation matrix approach is employed to derive the basic reproduction number, R0. Sensitivity analysis is conducted to identify the epidemiological parameters that most strongly influence disease transmission.
Main results
Local and global stability analyses demonstrate that the disease-free equilibrium is locally and globally asymptotically stable whenever R0 < 1, and a forward transcritical bifurcation occurs at R0 = 1. Numerical simulations validate these analytical findings and show the significant role of sustained public awareness in reducing disease transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that sustained public awareness plays a crucial role in reducing mpox transmission and mitigating epidemic outbreaks. This study introduces a novel mathematical framework that integrates public awareness into mpox transmission dynamics using fractional-order derivatives.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that combining behavioral awareness strategies with conventional public health interventions can substantially improve the long-term control of mpox. This has important implications for the development of targeted public health policies and awareness campaigns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mathematical modeling and does not include empirical data from real-world mpox outbreaks. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on theoretical analysis and simulations rather than direct observational or experimental data.

医学生对猴痘的认知和态度

Knowledge and attitude of monkeypox among medical students
作者： Batool M. MAHDI, Mohammed S. AL-IEDANI, Mohanad M. ABDULGHANI
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘病毒是中非和西非流行的人畜共患疾病之一,2022年少数暴发在多个国家传播。本研究旨在评估医学生对人猴痘的知识和态度。方法:于2022年10月至2023年5月进行横断面在线调查,评估281名医学生对猴痘的知识和态度。在伊拉克医学院。医学生参与者被告知该研究是匿名的,并通过在线谷歌表格问卷完成。结果:本研究的参与者总数为 281 名在阿尔金迪医学院不同阶段学习的学生。女性占63.7%,其余为男性(36.3%)。他们对这种疾病的了解是由病毒感染引起的(91.8%),并通过直接接触感染者(52.3%)和受感染的动物如猴子(29.9%)传播。储存宿主的学生信息为乌白眉猴(Cercocebusatys)(46.3%),患者临床表现为发热(36.7%)、皮疹(23.5%)、手掌和足底病变(17.4%),最少的是疲劳和淋巴结肿大。他们认为治疗通过使用抗病毒药物药物来治疗这种疾病(76.5%)和(23.5%)认为其是自限性疾病,而疫苗可以预防这种疾病(68%)和(32%)认为不存在有效的疫苗。他们对该疾病预后的态度是致命性疾病(60.1%),其余(39.9%)不是致命性疾病。结论:本研究表明大学生对疾病的知识和信息水平总体令人满意。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估医学生对猴痘的了解和态度,因为猴痘病毒是中非和西非流行的人畜共患疾病,暴发 将于 2022 年在全球传播。
设计与对象
2022 年 10 月至 2023 年 5 月,对伊拉克金迪医学院的 281 名医学生进行了一项横断面在线调查。参与者被告知该研究是匿名的,并通过在线谷歌表格问卷进行管理。
核心方法
该研究使用匿名在线谷歌表格问卷来评估医学生对猴痘的知识和态度。该调查于2022年10月至2023年5月进行。
主要结果
参与者中,女性占 63.7%,男性占 36.3%。学生正确地将猴痘识别为病毒感染(91.8%),并通过直接接触感染者(52.3%)和猴子等动物(29.9%)识别出传播。
解释与创新
该研究强调,医学生对猴痘(包括其病原体和 传播 途径)的了解总体令人满意,这在教育机构背景下是一个新颖的发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的教育干预措施可能有效提高人们对猴痘的了解,从而为疾病预防和控制的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于匿名在线调查的自我报告数据,这可能会引入偏差。由于横截面设计和缺乏纵向数据,证据强度中等。

Knowledge and attitude of monkeypox among medical students

Authors: Batool M. MAHDI, Mohammed S. AL-IEDANI, Mohanad M. ABDULGHANI
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Monkeypox virus is one of the zoonotic diseases which endemic in Central and Western Africa with a few outbreaks spreading in multiple countries in 2022. The aim of this study was to evaluate human monkeypox knowledge and attitude regarding human monkeypox among medical students.METHODS: A cross-sectional online survey was done from October 2022 to May 2023 to assess knowledge, attitude of Mpox among 281 medical students in Iraqi medical college. Medical students’ participants were informed that the study was anonymous and done through online Google form questionnaire.RESULTS: The total participants students in this study were 281 who studied at Al-kindy College of Medicine in different stages. Females were constituted 63.7% and the rest were males (36.3%). Their knowledge about this disease was caused by viral infection (91.8%) and transmitted through direct contact with infected person (52.3%) and infected animal like monkeys (29.9%). Students information about reservoir were Sooty Mangabey monkey (Cercocebusatys) (46.3%) with clinical manifestation of the patient were fever (36.7%), rash (23.5%), lesion in the palm and sole (17.4%), and the least ones were fatigue and lymphadenopathy. They thought that treatment of this disease through using antiviral drugs (76.5%) and (23.5%) believed its self-limiting disease and vaccine prevent this disease (68%) and (32%) supposed no effective vaccine was present. Their attitude about the prognosis of this disease was fatal disease (60.1%) and the rest (39.9%) was not fatal.CONCLUSIONS: The current study showed a generally satisfactory levels of knowledge and information’s regarding disease among university students.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate knowledge and attitude of monkeypox among medical students, as monkeypox virus is a zoonotic disease endemic in Central and Western Africa with outbreaks spreading globally in 2022.
Design and population
A cross-sectional online survey was conducted from October 2022 to May 2023 among 281 medical students at Al-kindy College of Medicine in Iraq. Participants were informed that the study was anonymous and administered through an online Google form questionnaire.
Core methods
The study used an anonymous online Google form questionnaire to assess knowledge and attitude of monkeypox among medical students. The survey was conducted from October 2022 to May 2023.
Main results
Among the participants, 63.7% were female and 36.3% were male. Students correctly identified monkeypox as a viral infection (91.8%) and recognized transmission through direct contact with infected persons (52.3%) and animals like monkeys (29.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that medical students have a generally satisfactory understanding of monkeypox, including its causative agent and transmission routes, which is a novel finding in the context of educational institutions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted educational interventions may be effective in improving knowledge about monkeypox, which could inform public health strategies for disease prevention and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data from an anonymous online survey, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the cross-sectional design and lack of longitudinal data.

ISG15差异调控Clade Ib和II MPXV感染

ISG15 differentially modulates Clade Ib and II MPXV infection
作者： Joseph Patrick McGrail, Irene Campaña Gómez, Denzel Eggermont, Antti Tuhkala, Salla Keskitalo, Antje Beling, Markku Varjosalo, Adolfo García Sastre, Gustavo Palacios, Francis Impens
摘要中文翻译2022年暴发期间Clade IIb猴痘病毒(MPXV)持续发生人传人传播,凸显了定义跨物种病毒适应性决定因素的必要性。ISG15编码一种具有抗病毒药物活性的泛素样修饰物,但其在MPXV感染和宿主适应中的作用仍不清楚。我们使用来自Clade Ib和II的代表性毒株,发现ISG15介导的限制存在显著的物种特异性差异。在小鼠细胞中,ISG15缺失主要增加病毒蛋白积累,对感染性病毒产量影响较小,表明其限制作用有限。相反,在人类细胞中,ISG15限制了近期流行毒株的复制,因为ISG15缺失会增强病毒生长,确立ISG15作为人类中的限制因子。ISGylome分析鉴定了潜在的病毒和宿主底物。这些发现揭示了ISG15依赖性的物种特异性MPXV感染控制,并支持一个模型,即啮齿动物作为允许的储存宿主,而人类则由于至少部分人类ISG15的抗病毒药物活性,表现出更严格的限制。我们研究了ISG15在人类和小鼠细胞中MPXV感染期间的作用。ISG15缺失在人类细胞中增加感染性病毒产量,但在小鼠细胞中改变病毒蛋白积累,且ISGylome分析鉴定了ISGylated病毒蛋白。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于证据A1和A2,2022年暴发期间Clade IIb MPXV持续发生人传人传播,凸显了定义跨物种病毒适应性决定因素的必要性,而ISG15在MPXV感染和宿主适应中的作用尚不明确,这促使了本研究的开展。
设计与对象
基于证据A3和A8,本研究在人类和小鼠细胞系中使用Clade Ib和Clade II MPXV的代表性毒株进行实验。
核心方法
基于证据A6,ISGylome分析鉴定了ISG15效应背后的潜在病毒和宿主底物。
主要结果
基于证据A4,在小鼠细胞中ISG15缺失主要增加病毒蛋白积累,对感染性病毒产量影响较小,表明其限制作用有限。
解释与创新
基于证据A5,在人类细胞中ISG15限制了近期流行毒株的复制,因为ISG15缺失会增强病毒生长,确立ISG15作为人类中的限制因子。
科研与公卫意义
基于证据 A7,这些发现揭示了 MPXV 感染的物种特异性 ISG15 依赖性控制,并支持了一种模型,其中啮齿类动物充当允许的 储存宿主,而人类由于 ISG15 活性而受到更多限制。
局限与证据强度
基于证据A8,本研究仅限于人类和小鼠细胞系中的体外实验,这限制了其对自然宿主动态的相关性,且仅基于摘要的证据(E1)导致了中等的置信度评估。

ISG15 differentially modulates Clade Ib and II MPXV infection

Authors: Joseph Patrick McGrail, Irene Campaña Gómez, Denzel Eggermont, Antti Tuhkala, Salla Keskitalo, Antje Beling, Markku Varjosalo, Adolfo García Sastre, Gustavo Palacios, Francis Impens
Original abstract (English)
The sustained human-to-human transmission of Clade IIb monkeypox virus (MPXV) during the 2022 outbreak underscores the need to define host determinants of viral fitness across species. ISG15 encodes a ubiquitin-like modifier with antiviral activity, yet its role in MPXV infection and host adaptation remains unclear. Using representative strains from Clades Ib and II, we identify a striking species-specific difference in ISG15-mediated restriction. In murine cells, ISG15 deficiency primarily increases viral protein accumulation with minimal effects on infectious viral production, indicating limited antiviral restriction. In contrast, in human cells, ISG15 restricts replication of recent epidemic strains since ISG15 deficiency enhances viral growth, establishing ISG15 as a restriction factor in humans. ISGylome profiling identifies candidate viral and host substrates underlying this effect. Together, these findings reveal species-specific ISG15-dependent control of MPXV infection and support a model in which rodents serve as permissive reservoirs while humans represent a more restrictive host due at least in part to the antiviral activity of human ISG15. We study ISG15 during MPXV infection in human and murine cells. ISG15 deficiency increases infectious virus production in human cells but alters viral protein accumulation in murine cells, and ISGylome analysis identifies ISGylated viral proteins.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Based on evidence A1 and A2, the sustained human‐to‐human transmission of Clade IIb MPXV during the 2022 outbreak highlighted the need to define host determinants of viral fitness across species, and the unclear role of ISG15 in MPXV infection and host adaptation motivated the study.
Design and population
Based on evidence A3 and A8, the investigation was performed in human and murine cell lines using representative strains of Clade Ib and Clade II MPXV.
Core methods
Based on evidence A6, ISGylome profiling identified candidate viral and host substrates underlying the ISG15 effect.
Main results
Based on evidence A4, in murine cells ISG15 deficiency primarily increases viral protein accumulation with minimal effect on infectious virus production, indicating limited antiviral restriction.
Interpretation and novelty
Based on evidence A5, in human cells ISG15 restricts replication of recent epidemic strains since ISG15 deficiency enhances viral growth, establishing ISG15 as a restriction factor in humans.
Scientific and public-health significance
Based on evidence A7, these findings reveal species‐specific ISG15‐dependent control of MPXV infection and support a model in which rodents serve as permissive reservoirs and humans are more restrictive due to ISG15 activity.
Limitations and evidence strength
Based on evidence A8, the study is limited to in vitro experiments in human and murine cell lines, which restricts its relevance to natural host dynamics, and the abstract‐only evidence (E1) leads to a moderate confidence assessment.

Journal: BMC Public Health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-04  |  DOI: 10.1186/s12889-026-29271-3  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

猴痘预防措施及相关因素在马来西亚南部沙捞越地区旅行相关环境中成年人中的研究:一项基于社区的横断面研究

Mpox preventive practices and associated factors among adults in a travel-connected setting in Southern Sarawak, Malaysia: a community-based cross-sectional study
作者： Faiz Mazlan, Sam Froze Jiee, Romano Ngui, Siong Hee Lim, Melvin Hsien Liang Chung, Fabiolla Lea
摘要中文翻译猴痘在非流行地区、旅行相关环境中仍然是重要的公共卫生问题,尽管当地没有持续的传播传播,仍可能发生输入病例。马来西亚南部沙捞越地区是一个社会人口多样性较高的地区,包括主要的行政和旅行中心基廷区,以及包括郊区和农村社区的帕达万次区。本研究旨在评估猴痘预防措施及与良好预防措施相关因素在南部沙捞越地区成年人中的情况。2025年1月至8月期间,对马来西亚南部沙捞越地区的442名成年人进行了基于社区的横断面研究。最小样本量通过单比例公式估算,并增加15%以考虑预期的非响应率,包括可能的拒绝参与、空置家庭和在实地招募期间无法参与的不合格受访者。研究采用了多阶段抽样设计:首先,有目的地选择了南部沙捞越地区的基廷区和帕达万次区,然后通过简单随机抽样选择居住社区,通过系统随机抽样选择家庭,对家庭成员进行资格筛查,并在每个家庭中有多名合格受访者时随机选择一名合格的成年人。使用结构化问卷评估社会人口特征、猴痘知识、态度、数字健康素养和预防措施。预防措施通过六个李克特量表项目进行测量,涵盖预防措施、隔离或检疫、避免人群聚集、口罩使用和手卫生、公众意识以及对地方政府行动的感知充分性,并将其分为良好和较差两类,得分≥22表示良好的预防措施。知识评估使用10个项目,得分≥7表示良好的知识。态度评估使用七个李克特量表项目,得分≥26表示积极态度。数字健康素养评估使用10个项目,改编自HLS19-DIGI框架,得分≥76.19%被归类为良好的数字健康素养。通过多变量二元逻辑回归分析了与良好预防措施相关的因素。在442名参与的成年人中,中位年龄为37.5岁(IQR:30–47),50.5%为男性,61.8%居住在城市地区。总体而言,95.0%的受访者报告了良好的猴痘预防措施,95.2%具有积极态度,尽管64.7%的受访者知识较差,56.3%具有良好的数字健康素养。最终模型统计学上显著(χ2(10) = 102.66,p < 0.001),显示良好的预防措施与年轻年龄、男性性别、自营职业、失业、积极态度和良好的数字健康素养独立相关。南部沙捞越地区的成年人报告了高水平的猴痘预防措施,但这种有利的预防措施与较差的猴痘相关知识和不均衡的数字健康素养并存。需要有针对性的风险沟通、加强数字健康素养、基于工作场所的指导以及本地适应的社区参与,以支持旅行相关环境中持续的预防措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估猴痘预防措施及与良好预防措施相关因素在马来西亚南部沙捞越地区成年人中的情况。猴痘在非流行地区、旅行相关环境中仍然是重要的公共卫生问题,尽管当地没有持续的传播传播,仍可能发生输入病例。
设计与对象
2025年1月至8月期间,对马来西亚南部沙捞越地区的442名成年人进行了基于社区的横断面研究。马来西亚南部沙捞越地区是一个社会人口多样性较高的地区,包括主要的行政和旅行中心基廷区,以及包括郊区和农村社区的帕达万次区。
核心方法
研究采用了多阶段抽样设计:首先,有目的地选择了南部沙捞越地区的基廷区和帕达万次区,然后通过简单随机抽样选择居住社区,通过系统随机抽样选择家庭,对家庭成员进行资格筛查,并在每个家庭中有多名合格受访者时随机选择一名合格的成年人。预防措施通过六个李克特量表项目进行测量,涵盖预防措施、隔离或检疫、避免人群聚集、口罩使用和手卫生、公众意识以及对地方政府行动的感知充分性,并将其分为良好和较差两类,得分≥22表示良好的预防措施。
主要结果
在442名参与的成年人中,中位年龄为37.5岁(IQR:30–47),50.5%为男性,61.8%居住在城市地区。总体而言,95.0%的受访者报告了良好的猴痘预防措施,95.2%具有积极态度,尽管64.7%的受访者知识较差,56.3%具有良好的数字健康素养。最终模型统计学上显著(χ2(10) = 102.66,p < 0.001),显示良好的预防措施与年轻年龄、男性性别、自营职业、失业、积极态度和良好的数字健康素养独立相关。
解释与创新
研究发现,高水平的猴痘预防措施与较差的猴痘相关知识和不均衡的数字健康素养并存。研究结果强调了在旅行相关环境中,需要有针对性的风险沟通、加强数字健康素养、基于工作场所的指导以及本地适应的社区参与,以支持持续的预防措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为旅行相关环境中针对性的风险沟通策略、数字健康素养项目以及社区参与活动提供信息,以改善猴痘预防措施。此外,研究还表明,提高知识和数字健康素养可能是维持高水平预防行为的关键。
局限与证据强度
本研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且可能存在自我报告偏差。证据强度中等,因为研究提供了关于预防措施及其相关因素的描述性数据,但未包括纵向或干预性数据。

Mpox preventive practices and associated factors among adults in a travel-connected setting in Southern Sarawak, Malaysia: a community-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Faiz Mazlan, Sam Froze Jiee, Romano Ngui, Siong Hee Lim, Melvin Hsien Liang Chung, Fabiolla Lea
Original abstract (English)
Mpox remains a relevant public health concern in non-endemic, travel-connected settings, where imported cases may occur despite the absence of sustained local transmission. Southern Sarawak, Malaysia, is a socio-demographically diverse region that includes Kuching District, a major administrative and travel hub, and Padawan sub-district, which includes peri-urban and rural communities. This study aimed to assess Mpox preventive practices and factors associated with good preventive practices among adults in Southern Sarawak. A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January to August 2025 among 442 adults in Southern Sarawak, Malaysia. The minimum sample size was estimated using a single-proportion formula and increased by 15% to account for anticipated non-response, including possible refusals, vacant households, and ineligible respondents unavailable during field recruitment. A multistage sampling design was used: Kuching District and Padawan sub-district within Southern Sarawak were purposively selected, residential localities were selected using simple random sampling, households were selected using systematic random sampling, household members were screened for eligibility, and one eligible adult was randomly selected when more than one eligible respondent was present in a household. A structured questionnaire assessed sociodemographic characteristics, Mpox knowledge, attitudes, digital health literacy, and preventive practice. Preventive practice was measured using six Likert-type items covering precautionary measures, quarantine or isolation, avoidance of crowds, mask use and hand hygiene, public awareness, and perceived adequacy of local authority action, and dichotomised as good and poor, with scores ≥ 22 indicating good preventive practice. Knowledge was assessed using 10 items; scores ≥ 7 indicated good knowledge. Attitude was assessed using seven Likert-scale items; scores ≥ 26 indicated a positive attitude. Digital health literacy was assessed using 10 items adapted from the HLS19-DIGI framework, with scores ≥ 76.19% classified as good digital health literacy. Factors associated with good preventive practice were examined using multivariable binary logistic regression. Among the 442 adults who participated, the median age was 37.5 years (IQR: 30–47), 50.5% were male, and 61.8% resided in urban areas. Overall, 95.0% reported good Mpox preventive practice and 95.2% had a positive attitude, although 64.7% had poor Mpox knowledge and 56.3% had good digital health literacy. The final model was statistically significant (χ2(10) = 102.66, p < 0.001) and showed that good preventive practice was independently associated with younger age, male gender, self-employment, unemployment, positive attitude, and good digital health literacy. Adults in Southern Sarawak reported high levels of Mpox preventive practices, but this favourable practice coexisted with poor Mpox-specific knowledge and uneven digital health literacy. Targeted risk communication, strengthening digital health literacy, workplace-based guidance, and locally adapted community engagement are needed to support sustained preventive practice in travel-connected settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess Mpox preventive practices and factors associated with good preventive practices among adults in Southern Sarawak, Malaysia. Mpox remains a relevant public health concern in non-endemic, travel-connected settings, where imported cases may occur despite the absence of sustained local transmission.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January to August 2025 among 442 adults in Southern Sarawak, Malaysia. Southern Sarawak, Malaysia, is a socio-demographically diverse region that includes Kuching District, a major administrative and travel hub, and Padawan sub-district, which includes peri-urban and rural communities.
Core methods
The study used a multistage sampling design: Kuching District and Padawan sub-district within Southern Sarawak were purposively selected, residential localities were selected using simple random sampling, households were selected using systematic random sampling, household members were screened for eligibility, and one eligible adult was randomly selected when more than one eligible respondent was present in a household. Preventive practice was measured using six Likert-type items covering precautionary measures, quarantine or isolation, avoidance of crowds, mask use and hand hygiene, public awareness, and perceived adequacy of local authority action, and dichotomised as good and poor, with scores ≥ 22 indicating good preventive practice.
Main results
Among the 442 adults who participated, the median age was 37.5 years (IQR: 30–47), 50.5% were male, and 61.8% resided in urban areas. Overall, 95.0% reported good Mpox preventive practice and 95.2% had a positive attitude, although 64.7% had poor Mpox knowledge and 56.3% had good digital health literacy. The final model was statistically significant (χ2(10) = 102.66, p < 0.001) and showed that good preventive practice was independently associated with younger age, male gender, self-employment, unemployment, positive attitude, and good digital health literacy.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that high levels of Mpox preventive practices coexisted with poor Mpox-specific knowledge and uneven digital health literacy. The findings highlight the need for targeted risk communication, strengthening digital health literacy, workplace-based guidance, and locally adapted community engagement to support sustained preventive practice in travel-connected settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted risk communication strategies, digital health literacy programs, and community engagement efforts in travel-connected settings to improve Mpox preventive practices. They also suggest that improving knowledge and digital health literacy may be critical to maintaining high levels of preventive behavior.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and by the potential for self-reporting bias. The evidence is moderate, as the study provides descriptive data on preventive practices and associated factors but does not include longitudinal or interventional data.

猴痘与乌干达马萨卡区多个性伴侣及HIV共感染,2025年2月至4月

Mpox outbreak associated with multiple sexual partnerships and HIV coinfection in Masaka District, Uganda, February–April 2025
作者： Gertrude Abbo, Richard Migisha, Emmanuel Okiror Okello, Bridget Ainembabazi, Stella Martha Migamba, Charles Kasozi, Lilian Bulage, Benon Kwesiga, Steven Ndugwa Kabwama, Alex Riolexus Ario
摘要中文翻译背景 乌干达在2025年初经历了猴痘病例的显著增加,其中在中部地区出现了明显的聚集现象。马萨卡区报告了约4,800例确认的猴痘病例和37例死亡,占全国负担的约3%。我们对马萨卡区的暴发进行了调查,以描述其流行病学并确定相关风险因素。方法 我们进行了暴发调查,采用描述性流行病学分析和非配对病例对照研究。疑似病例定义为2025年2月至4月期间,马萨卡区居民出现皮疹伴发热,并至少伴有淋巴结肿大、头痛、肌肉疼痛、背痛或疲劳等症状。确诊病例为PCR阳性。我们纳入了86例确诊病例和172例社区对照(1:2比例)。通过结构化问卷收集了有关社会人口学特征、就医行为和潜在暴露的数据。多变量逻辑回归分析确定了独立风险因素。结果 在86例确诊病例中,45例(52.3%)为男性,41例(47.7%)为女性。受影响最严重的年龄组为25-34岁(每10,000人中有24例),而布卡卡塔子县(一个渔民社区)的居民攻击率最高(每10,000人中有5例)。超过一半(56%)的病例为HIV阳性。独立风险因素包括HIV感染(aOR:5.4;95% CI:2.3–12.5)、有多个性伴侣(aOR:7.3;95% CI:3.5–15.3)以及与有皮疹者密切接触(aOR:3.2;95% CI:1.4–6.9)。结论 猴痘 暴发主要影响年轻人,其传播主要由高风险性网络传播和HIV感染驱动。加强针对性网络的HIV项目中猴痘的筛查、预防和护理,可以中断传播并减轻未来的暴发。

Mpox outbreak associated with multiple sexual partnerships and HIV coinfection in Masaka District, Uganda, February–April 2025

Authors: Gertrude Abbo, Richard Migisha, Emmanuel Okiror Okello, Bridget Ainembabazi, Stella Martha Migamba, Charles Kasozi, Lilian Bulage, Benon Kwesiga, Steven Ndugwa Kabwama, Alex Riolexus Ario
Original abstract (English)
Background Uganda experienced a substantial increase in mpox cases in early 2025, with notable clustering in central regions. Masaka district reported approximately 4,800 confirmed mpox cases and 37 deaths, contributing approximately 3% of the national burden. We investigated an outbreak in Masaka District to describe its epidemiology and identify associated risk factors. Methods We conducted an outbreak investigation with a descriptive epidemiological analysis and an unmatched case–control study. A suspected case was defined as onset of rash with fever and at least one additional symptom (lymphadenopathy, headache, myalgia, back pain, or fatigue) in a Masaka District resident during February–April 2025. Confirmed cases were PCR-positive. We enrolled 86 confirmed cases and 172 neighborhood controls (1:2 ratio). Data on sociodemographic characteristics, care-seeking behavior, and potential exposures were collected using structured questionnaires. Multivariable logistic regression identified independent risk factors. Results Of the 86 confirmed cases, 45 (52.3%) were male and 41 (47.7%) were female. The most affected age group was 25-34 years (24 per 10,000 population), and residents of Bukakata Sub-county, a fisherfolk community, had the highest attack rate (5 per 10,000). Over half (56%) were HIV positive. Independent risk factors included HIV infection (aOR: 5.4; 95% CI: 2.3–12.5), having multiple sexual partners (aOR: 7.3; 95% CI: 3.5–15.3), and close contact with an individual with a rash (aOR: 3.2; 95% CI: 1.4–6.9). Conclusion The Mpox outbreak affected young adults, driven by high-risk sexual networks and underlying HIV infection. Strengthening integration of Mpox screening, prevention, and care within HIV programs targeting sexual networks could interrupt transmission and mitigate future outbreaks.

Question and background
We investigated an outbreak in Masaka District to describe its epidemiology and identify associated risk factors.
Design and population
We enrolled 86 confirmed cases and 172 neighborhood controls (1:2 ratio).
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression identified independent risk factors.
Main results
Results Of the 86 confirmed cases, 45 (52.3%) were male and 41 (47.7%) were female.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusion The Mpox outbreak affected young adults, driven by high-risk sexual networks and underlying HIV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening integration of Mpox screening, prevention, and care within HIV programs targeting sexual networks could interrupt transmission and mitigate future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
Over half (56%) were HIV positive.

基于神经网络的猴痘患者状态建模:利用临床和人口统计学特征

Neural network-based modeling of monkeypox patient status using clinical and demographic characteristics
作者： Hazem Al-Najjar, Nadia Al-Rousan, Dania Al-Najjar, Hamzeh F. Assous, Hazem Al‐Najjar, Nadia Al‐Rousan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 猴痘(猴痘)已成为日益严重的公共卫生问题,需要有效的工具来进行早期患者风险评估和临床决策。本研究旨在利用多层感知器(MLP)神经网络开发一种基于人工智能(AI)的机器学习框架,分析从11个非流行区国家收集的猴痘数据集,识别最重要的临床症状,探讨旅行史、住院、隔离和感染状态之间的关系,并预测猴痘患者的住院和隔离状态。方法 从2022年5月6日至5月29日公开的世卫组织(WHO)报告中,纳入了来自11个非流行区国家的365例非严重猴痘病例。患者被分为两类:住院患者和隔离患者。临床数据被转换为二元虚拟变量,人口统计学和旅行史数据被作为特征添加。通过交叉列联表和描述性可视化分析症状模式及临床和人口统计学变量之间的关系。随后,开发了一个监督式AI基于的MLP神经网络来预测住院和隔离状态,并通过准确率、召回率、漏诊率(FOR)和F1分数评估模型性能。结果 分析中报告的最常见症状包括生殖器溃疡、发热和皮疹。研究中包含的11个国家报告了不同的症状模式,其中阿根廷表现出最高的症状多样性,每例病例最多记录四种症状。所提出的MLP神经网络在住院预测的训练总体准确率为94.7%,测试总体准确率为90.7%。对于隔离预测的每个患者状态分类任务,模型在训练阶段的准确率为94.2%,在测试阶段的准确率为90.1%。结论 所提出的基于人工智能的多层感知器(MLP)神经网络为利用临床和人口统计学变量预测猴痘患者的住院和隔离状态提供了一种有效的方法。症状分布因国家而异,表明疾病表现存在地区异质性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘(猴痘)已成为日益严重的公共卫生问题,需要有效的工具来进行早期患者风险评估和临床决策。
设计与对象
本研究旨在利用多层感知器(MLP)神经网络开发一种基于人工智能(AI)的机器学习框架,分析从11个非流行区国家收集的猴痘数据集,识别最重要的临床症状,探讨旅行史、住院、隔离和感染状态之间的关系,并预测猴痘患者的住院和隔离状态。
核心方法
从2022年5月6日至5月29日公开的世卫组织(WHO)报告中,纳入了来自11个非流行区国家的365例非严重猴痘病例。临床数据被转换为二元虚拟变量,人口统计学和旅行史数据被作为特征添加。随后,开发了一个监督式AI基于的MLP神经网络来预测住院和隔离状态,并通过准确率、召回率、漏诊率(FOR)和F1分数评估模型性能。
主要结果
分析中报告的最常见症状包括生殖器溃疡、发热和皮疹。研究中包含的11个国家报告了不同的症状模式,其中阿根廷表现出最高的症状多样性,每例病例最多记录四种症状。所提出的MLP神经网络在住院预测的训练总体准确率为94.7%,测试总体准确率为90.7%。对于隔离预测的每个患者状态分类任务,模型在训练阶段的准确率为94.2%,在测试阶段的准确率为90.1%。
解释与创新
所提出的基于人工智能的多层感知器(MLP)神经网络为利用临床和人口统计学变量预测猴痘患者的住院和隔离状态提供了一种有效的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以用于开发预测猴痘患者状态的工具,有助于临床决策和患者护理资源的分配。
局限与证据强度
症状分布因国家而异,表明疾病表现存在地区异质性。由于研究基于公开的WHO报告,且未包含详细的生物学或病毒学分析,证据强度中等。

Neural network-based modeling of monkeypox patient status using clinical and demographic characteristics

Authors: Hazem Al-Najjar, Nadia Al-Rousan, Dania Al-Najjar, Hamzeh F. Assous, Hazem Al‐Najjar, Nadia Al‐Rousan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Monkeypox (Mpox) has emerged as a growing public health concern, requiring effective tools for early patient risk assessment and clinical decision-making. This study aimed to develop an artificial intelligence (AI)-based machine learning framework using a multilayer perceptron (MLP) neural network to analyze a Mpox dataset collected from 11 non-endemic countries, identify the most significant clinical symptoms, investigate the relationships among travel history, hospitalization, isolation, and infection status, and predict the hospitalization and isolation status of Mpox patients. Methods A total of 365 non-severe Mpox cases from 11 non-endemic countries were included in the analysis provided in publicly available World Health Organization (WHO) reports from May 6 to May 29, 2022. Patients were classified into two groups: Hospitalized and isolated patients. Clinical data were converted to binary dummy variables and demographic and travel-history data were added as features. The data were analyzed using cross-tabulation and descriptive visualization for symptom patterns and relationships between clinical and demographic variables. A supervised AI-based MLP neural network was then developed to predict the status of hospitalization and isolation, and the performance of the model was assessed by accuracy, recall, false omission rate (FOR) and F1 score. Results The symptoms most commonly reported in the analysis included genital ulcers, fever, and lesions. The eleven countries included in the study reported varying symptom patterns, with Argentina showing the highest symptom diversity, with up to four symptoms recorded per case.. The overall training accuracy of the proposed MLP neural network for the prediction of hospitalization was 94.7% and the overall testing accuracy was 90.7%. The model showed good predictive performance for each patient status classification task with an accuracy of 94.2% at training phase and 90.1% at test phase for isolation prediction. Conclusion The proposed AI-based multilayer perceptron (MLP) neural network provides an effective approach for predicting the hospitalization and isolation status of Mpox patients using clinical and demographic variables. Symptom distributions varied across countries, indicating regional heterogeneity in disease presentation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox (Mpox) has emerged as a growing public health concern, requiring effective tools for early patient risk assessment and clinical decision-making.
Design and population
This study aimed to develop an artificial intelligence (AI)-based machine learning framework using a multilayer perceptron (MLP) neural network to analyze a Mpox dataset collected from 11 non-endemic countries, identify the most significant clinical symptoms, investigate the relationships among travel history, hospitalization, isolation, and infection status, and predict the hospitalization and isolation status of Mpox patients.

A total of 365 non-severe Mpox cases from 11 non-endemic countries were included in the analysis provided in publicly available World Health Organization (WHO) reports from May 6 to May 29, 2022. Clinical data were converted to binary dummy variables and demographic and travel-history data were added as features. A supervised AI-based MLP neural network was then developed to predict the status of hospitalization and isolation, and the performance of the model was assessed by accuracy, recall, false omission rate (FOR) and F1 score.
Main results
The symptoms most commonly reported in the analysis included genital ulcers, fever, and lesions. The eleven countries included in the study reported varying symptom patterns, with Argentina showing the highest symptom diversity, with up to four symptoms recorded per case. The overall training accuracy of the proposed MLP neural network for the prediction of hospitalization was 94.7% and the overall testing accuracy was 90.7%. The model showed good predictive performance for each patient status classification task with an accuracy of 94.2% at training phase and 90.1% at test phase for isolation prediction.
Interpretation and novelty
The proposed AI-based multilayer perceptron (MLP) neural network provides an effective approach for predicting the hospitalization and isolation status of Mpox patients using clinical and demographic variables.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of predictive tools for Mpox patient status, aiding in clinical decision-making and resource allocation for patient care.
Limitations and evidence strength
Symptom distributions varied across countries, indicating regional heterogeneity in disease presentation. The evidence is moderate as the study is based on publicly available WHO reports and does not include detailed biological or virological analysis.
