猴痘病毒与柯萨奇B病毒在一名HIV控制良好的跨性别女性中的共感染

Mpox Coinfection with Coxsackie B Virus in a Transgender Host with Well-Controlled HIV
作者： Ranel Thaker, Ayesha Khan, Tommy Ojukwu Perdomo, Leilah Tisheh,
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘病毒(以前称为猴痘)是一种双链DNA病毒,其全球发病率正在上升,尤其是在男男性行为者(MSM)和HIV感染者中。已有报告指出与其他病毒如HSV和水痘病毒的共感染情况,但至今尚未有与柯萨奇病毒共感染的记录。病例:我们报告了一例30岁HIV控制良好的跨性别女性中首次发现的猴痘病毒与柯萨奇B病毒共感染病例。患者出现了广泛性疼痛性皮疹、肛周病变、直肠疼痛和全身症状。体格检查发现了猴痘病毒的特征性水疱-脓疱样病变,实验室检测通过PCR确认了猴痘病毒的感染,并通过阳性柯萨奇病毒检测确认了共感染。患者在接受支持性治疗后病情好转,并在隔离预防措施、对高风险密切接触者进行JYNNEOS疫苗的暴露后预防以及适当的随访建议下出院。讨论:猴痘病毒和柯萨奇病毒具有重叠的临床特征,这可能使早期诊断变得复杂,尤其是在免疫功能低下的患者中。尽管患者的HIV得到了良好控制,但其免疫特征表明存在潜在的免疫激活,这可能影响疾病严重程度和表现。该病例强调了在MSM和HIV阳性人群中出现皮疹和全身症状时,保持广泛鉴别诊断的重要性。结论:该病例突显了对高风险患者进行全面诊断评估的必要性,包括考虑共感染的可能性。在免疫功能低下的群体中,常规筛查和预防策略,包括JYNNEOS疫苗,仍需进一步研究。局限性与证据强度:本研究基于单一病例,限制了研究结果的普遍适用性。证据仅限于临床观察和实验室检测,研究结果的可信度中等。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒(以前称为猴痘)是一种双链DNA病毒,其全球发病率正在上升,尤其是在男男性行为者(MSM)和HIV感染者中。本研究旨在报告一例在一名HIV控制良好的跨性别女性中发生的猴痘病毒与柯萨奇B病毒共感染病例。
设计与对象
本研究描述了一名30岁HIV控制良好的跨性别女性患者,其出现广泛性疼痛性皮疹、肛周病变、直肠疼痛和全身症状。该研究在临床环境中进行,聚焦于一名已知免疫功能低下的个体。
核心方法
体格检查发现了猴痘病毒的特征性水疱-脓疱样病变,实验室检测通过PCR确认了猴痘病毒的感染,并通过阳性柯萨奇病毒检测确认了共感染。
主要结果
患者被确认感染了猴痘病毒和柯萨奇B病毒。截至目前,尚未有猴痘病毒与柯萨奇B病毒共感染的记录。
解释与创新
猴痘病毒和柯萨奇B病毒具有重叠的临床特征,这可能使早期诊断变得复杂,尤其是在免疫功能低下的患者中。该病例强调了在高风险人群中出现皮疹和全身症状时,保持广泛鉴别诊断的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该病例突显了对高风险患者进行全面诊断评估的必要性,包括考虑共感染的可能性,这可能对免疫功能低下的个体的治疗和管理策略产生影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于单一病例,限制了研究结果的普遍适用性。证据仅限于临床观察和实验室检测,研究结果的可信度中等。

Mpox Coinfection with Coxsackie B Virus in a Transgender Host with Well-Controlled HIV

Authors: Ranel Thaker, Ayesha Khan, Tommy Ojukwu Perdomo, Leilah Tisheh,
Original abstract (English)
Background: Mpox (formerly monkeypox) is a double-stranded DNA virus with increasing global prevalence, particularly among men who have sex with men (MSM) and individuals living with HIV. Co-infections with other viruses such as HSV and Varicella have been reported, but no documented cases with Coxsackievirus exist to date. Case: We report the first known case of co-infection with Mpox and Coxsackie B virus in a 30-year-old transgender female with well-controlled HIV. The patient presented with a diffuse painful rash, perianal lesions, rectal pain, and constitutional symptoms. Physical examination revealed characteristic vesiculopustular lesions of Mpox, and laboratory testing confirmed co-infection with Mpox by PCR and Coxsackie B virus by positive serology. The patient improved with supportive care and was discharged with isolation precautions, post-exposure prophylaxis for high-risk close contacts with the JYNNEOS vaccine, and appropriate follow-up recommendations. Discussion: Mpox and Coxsackievirus share overlapping clinical features, complicating early diagnosis, particularly in immunocompromised hosts. While the patient’s HIV was well controlled, her immune profile suggested underlying immune activation, potentially influencing disease severity and presentation. This case highlights the importance of maintaining a broad differential in MSM and HIV-positive populations presenting with a rash and systemic symptoms. Conclusion: This case underscores the need for comprehensive diagnostic evaluation in high-risk patients, including consideration of co-infections. Routine screening and preventive strategies, including vaccination, warrant further study in immunocompromised populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox (formerly monkeypox) is a double-stranded DNA virus with increasing global prevalence, particularly among men who have sex with men (MSM) and individuals living with HIV. The study aimed to report a case of co-infection with Mpox and Coxsackie B virus in a transgender host with well-controlled HIV.
Design and population
This case report describes a single patient, a 30-year-old transgender female with well-controlled HIV, who presented with a diffuse painful rash, perianal lesions, rectal pain, and constitutional symptoms. The study was conducted in a clinical setting and focused on a specific individual with a known immunocompromised status.
Core methods
Physical examination identified characteristic vesiculopustular lesions of Mpox, and laboratory testing confirmed co-infection with Mpox via PCR and Coxsackie B virus via positive serology.
Main results
The patient was confirmed to have co-infection with Mpox and Coxsackie B virus. No documented cases of co-infection with Coxsackie B virus and Mpox exist to date.
Interpretation and novelty
Mpox and Coxsackie B virus share overlapping clinical features, which may complicate early diagnosis, especially in immunocompromised hosts. This case highlights the importance of maintaining a broad differential in high-risk populations presenting with a rash and systemic symptoms.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case underscores the need for comprehensive diagnostic evaluation in high-risk patients, including consideration of co-infections, which may have implications for surveillance and management strategies in immunocompromised individuals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single case report, which limits the generalizability of findings. The evidence is limited to clinical observations and laboratory testing, and the confidence in the findings is moderate.

世界卫生组织与疾病控制和预防中心 猴痘病毒 分子检测实时 PCR 检测的诊断性能比较

Comparison of diagnostic performance between the World Health Organization and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention real-time PCR assays for molecular detection of the mpox virus
作者： Darwin Paredes-Núñez, Silvia Salgado, Martha Sánchez, José Echevarría, Johanna Parrales, Andrés Tinizaray, Andrea Belén Salcedo Maldonado, Solón Alberto Orlando, Miguel Angel Garcia Bereguiain
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 (猴痘) 在全球的出现和传播凸显了对准确可靠的分子诊断工具的需求。实时PCR仍然是检测猴痘病毒 (MPXV)的参考方法;然而,可用协议之间的差异可能会影响诊断性能。本研究的目的是使用厄瓜多尔的临床样本,比较美国疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC) 实时 PCR 检测与世界卫生组织 (WHO) 参考分子方案的诊断准确性。对疑似 猴痘 病例的 300 份标本进行回顾性分析,包括病灶拭子、水泡样本和血清,其中 150 份先前经 WHO 检测确认为 MPXV 阳性,150 份为 MPXV 阴性。使用标准化方案提取 DNA,并使用 CDC 检测对所有样本进行测试。CDC 协议正确识别了 300 个样本中的 291 个,产生 141 个真阳性、150 个真阴性、9 个假阴性,没有假阳性。总体一致性为 97% (95% CI: 94–98),敏感性为 94% (95% CI: 89–97),特异性为 100% (95% CI: 98–100)。阳性预测值为100%,阴性预测值为94%(95% CI:90-97)。获得的科恩卡帕系数为 0.94(95% CI:0.90-0.98)。这些研究结果表明,与 WHO 参考方案相比,CDC 实时 PCR 检测为 MPXV 检测提供了高诊断准确性和出色的特异性,支持将 CDC 和 WHO 实时 PCR 检测用作临床和 监测 设置中的可靠诊断工具。重要性 MPXV 在全球的出现和传播凸显了对准确 PCR 诊断工具的需求。这是第一项对美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 实时 PCR 检测和世界卫生组织 (WHO) 的 MPXV PCR 方案进行比较的研究,这两个机构是全球公认的传染病参考文献 诊断。我们表明,两种 PCR 方案对于 MPXV 诊断 都非常可靠,并且可以在全球任何实验室中实施。

猴痘病毒(MPXV)在全球范围内的出现和传播凸显了开发准确的PCR诊断工具的必要性。

对疑似 猴痘 病例的 300 份标本进行回顾性分析,包括病灶拭子、水泡样本和血清,其中 150 份先前经 WHO 检测确认为 MPXV 阳性,150 份为 MPXV 阴性。
核心方法
使用标准化方案提取 DNA,并使用 CDC 检测对所有样本进行测试。
主要结果
CDC 协议正确识别了 300 个样本中的 291 个,产生 141 个真阳性、150 个真阴性、9 个假阴性,没有假阳性。

这是第一项研究,比较了来自美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)实时PCR检测方法和世界卫生组织(WHO)的猴痘病毒(MPXV)PCR协议,这两个机构在全球传染病领域被公认为权威参考。

Comparison of diagnostic performance between the World Health Organization and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention real-time PCR assays for molecular detection of the mpox virus

Authors: Darwin Paredes-Núñez, Silvia Salgado, Martha Sánchez, José Echevarría, Johanna Parrales, Andrés Tinizaray, Andrea Belén Salcedo Maldonado, Solón Alberto Orlando, Miguel Angel Garcia Bereguiain
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The global emergence and spread of monkeypox (mpox) have underscored the need for accurate and reliable molecular diagnostic tools. Real-time PCR remains the reference method for detecting mpox virus (MPXV); however, variations among available protocols may affect diagnostic performance. The objective of this study was to compare the diagnostic accuracy of the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) of the USA real-time PCR assay with the World Health Organization (WHO) reference molecular protocol, using clinical samples from Ecuador. A retrospective analysis was conducted on 300 specimens from suspected mpox cases, including lesion swabs, vesicular samples, and serum, of which 150 were previously confirmed as MPXV-positive and 150 as MPXV-negative by the WHO assay. DNA was extracted using a standardized protocol, and all samples were tested with the CDC assay. The CDC protocol correctly identified 291 of 300 samples, yielding 141 true positives, 150 true negatives, 9 false negatives, and no false positives. The overall agreement was 97% (95% CI: 94–98), with a sensitivity of 94% (95% CI: 89–97) and a specificity of 100% (95% CI: 98–100). The positive predictive value was 100%, and the negative predictive value was 94% (95% CI: 90–97). The Cohen’s kappa coefficient obtained was 0.94 (95% CI: 0.90-0.98). These findings demonstrate that the CDC real-time PCR assay provides high diagnostic accuracy and excellent specificity for MPXV detection compared with the WHO reference protocol, supporting the use of both CDC and WHO real-time PCR assays as reliable diagnostic tools in clinical and surveillance settings. IMPORTANCE The global emergence and spread of MPXV have underscored the need for accurate PCR diagnostic tools. This is the first study to address a comparison between the PCR protocols for MPXV from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) of the USA real-time PCR assay and the World Health Organization (WHO), two institutions globally acknowledged as references for infectious disease diagnosis. We show that both PCR protocols are very reliable for MPXV diagnosis and could be implemented for any laboratory worldwide indistinctly.

IMPORTANCE The global emergence and spread of MPXV have underscored the need for accurate PCR diagnostic tools.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 300 specimens from suspected mpox cases, including lesion swabs, vesicular samples, and serum, of which 150 were previously confirmed as MPXV-positive and 150 as MPXV-negative by the WHO assay.
Core methods
DNA was extracted using a standardized protocol, and all samples were tested with the CDC assay.
Main results
The CDC protocol correctly identified 291 of 300 samples, yielding 141 true positives, 150 true negatives, 9 false negatives, and no false positives.
Interpretation and novelty
This is the first study to address a comparison between the PCR protocols for MPXV from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) of the USA real-time PCR assay and the World Health Organization (WHO), two institutions globally acknowledged as references for infectious disease diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
ABSTRACT The global emergence and spread of monkeypox (mpox) have underscored the need for accurate and reliable molecular diagnostic tools.
Limitations and evidence strength
The Cohen’s kappa coefficient obtained was 0.94 (95% CI: 0.90-0.98).

猴痘病毒在男男性行为者人群中的传播动力学:一种数学建模方法

MONKEYPOX TRANSMISSION DYNAMICS IN A POPULATION OF MEN WHO HAVE SEX WITH MEN: A MATHEMATICAL MODELLING APPROACH
作者： Adepoju O. A., Ibrahim H. O., Awojugbagbe J. I., Olanrewaju S. O., Salahu W. O., Adejumo A. E., O. A. Adepoju, W.O. Salahu
摘要中文翻译本研究利用一种包含两种疫苗剂量给药的数学模型,探讨猴痘病毒在人类人群中的传播。该模型的基本性质,包括解的非负性和有界性,已被确立,以确保其生物学和流行病学意义。通过使用下一代矩阵算子推导出有效再生数,这一重要阈值用于评估疾病传播,并分析模型平衡状态存在的条件。此外,对有效再生数进行了归一化前向敏感性分析,以识别对人群中疾病传播有显著影响的参数。研究结果为制定有效的控制策略和适当的干预措施提供了有用的见解,以减少猴痘传播在人群IARJSET中的传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究采用数学模型探讨猴痘病毒在人类人群中的传播,以理解传播动力学并为控制策略提供依据。
设计与对象
本研究采用数学建模方法,探讨男男性行为者人群中猴痘传播的动力学,重点关注疾病传播和控制措施的条件。
核心方法
本研究采用一种包含两种疫苗剂量给药的数学模型,确立模型的基本性质,利用下一代矩阵算子推导有效再生数,并进行归一化前向敏感性分析以识别影响参数。
主要结果
研究结果为制定有效的控制策略和适当的干预措施提供了有用的见解,以减少猴痘传播在人群中的传播。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为对控制策略和干预措施的见解,这些见解在应用于特定研究人群方面具有新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为猴痘在高风险人群,特别是男男性行为者中的监测和干预策略提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于数学模型,未提供实证数据,这限制了证据的强度为中等。

MONKEYPOX TRANSMISSION DYNAMICS IN A POPULATION OF MEN WHO HAVE SEX WITH MEN: A MATHEMATICAL MODELLING APPROACH

Authors: Adepoju O. A., Ibrahim H. O., Awojugbagbe J. I., Olanrewaju S. O., Salahu W. O., Adejumo A. E., O. A. Adepoju, W.O. Salahu
Original abstract (English)
The spread of monkeypox within a human population is investigated in this study using a mathematical model that incorporates the administration of two vaccine doses.The model's fundamental properties including the nonnegativity and boundedness of its solutions are established to ensure its biological and epidemiological significance.The effective reproduction number, an important threshold for assessing disease transmission, is derived using the next generation matrix operator and used to analyse the conditions for the existence of equilibrium states of the model.In addition, the normalized forward sensitivity is performed on the effective reproduction number to identify parameters that strongly influence the spread of the disease in the population.The results provides useful insights into effective control strategies and appropriate intervention measures in reducing monkeypox transmission within the population IARJSET
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the spread of monkeypox within a human population using a mathematical model to understand transmission dynamics and inform control strategies.
Design and population
A mathematical modelling approach is used to study monkeypox transmission dynamics in a population of men who have sex with men, focusing on the conditions for disease spread and control measures.
Core methods
The study employs a mathematical model that incorporates the administration of two vaccine doses, establishes fundamental properties of the model, derives the effective reproduction number using the next generation matrix operator, and performs normalized forward sensitivity analysis to identify influential parameters.
Main results
The results provide useful insights into effective control strategies and appropriate intervention measures in reducing monkeypox transmission within the population.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as providing insights into control strategies and intervention measures, which are novel in their application to the specific population studied.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and intervention strategies for monkeypox in populations at risk, particularly men who have sex with men.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model and does not provide empirical data, which limits the strength of the evidence to moderate.

国际猴痘流行病学特征的多变量分析:刚果民主共和国的位置

ANALYSE MULTIVARIÉE DES PROFILS ÉPIDÉMIOLOGIQUES DE LA MPOX À L’ÉCHELLE INTERNATIONALE PAR ANALYSE EN COMPOSANTES PRINCIPALES : Positionnement de la République Démocratique du Congo
作者： Sonia Arlette Nsenia Mpasi, Rostin Matendo Mabela, Jean Otshudi Otshudiakoy
摘要中文翻译本文采用主成分分析(PCA)对国际猴痘流行病学特征进行了描述性多变量分析。数据集来源于Our World in Data 猴痘数据库,该数据库基于世界卫生组织数据,涵盖2022年5月1日至2026年2月28日的时间段。在去除区域和全球汇总数据、检查ISO代码、处理缺失值并构建国家层面指标后,保留了144个国家和地区。分析了15个变量,包括累积负担、死亡率、峰值强度、时间分散性和传播持续时间。巴特利特球形度检验(χ2 = 2972.55;p < 0.001)和总体KMO指数(0.668)支持了探索性PCA的应用。前三个主成分解释了总方差的71.55%。第一个主成分主要代表观察到的强度和严重程度,第二个主成分代表传播的持续时间和持久性,第三个主成分则代表病例负担与死亡负担之间的对比。本研究的贡献在于将每日监测系列数据转化为可重复的国家-指标矩阵,并将刚果民主共和国定位在国际因子空间中。刚果民主共和国具有较高的PC1得分和正的PC2得分,与高报告负担和持续传播的特征一致。这种解释仍保持描述性,并依赖于国家监测系统的质量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本文采用主成分分析(PCA)对国际猴痘流行病学特征进行了描述性多变量分析。本研究旨在将刚果民主共和国定位在国际猴痘疾病模式的因子空间中。
设计与对象
数据集来源于Our World in Data 猴痘数据库,该数据库基于世界卫生组织数据,涵盖2022年5月1日至2026年2月28日的时间段。在去除区域和全球汇总数据、检查ISO代码、处理缺失值并构建国家层面指标后,保留了144个国家和地区。
核心方法
分析了15个变量,包括累积负担、死亡率、峰值强度、时间分散性和传播持续时间。
主要结果
前三个主成分解释了总方差的71.55%。刚果民主共和国具有较高的PC1得分和正的PC2得分,与高报告负担和持续传播的特征一致。
解释与创新
本研究的贡献在于将每日监测系列数据转化为可重复的国家-指标矩阵,并将刚果民主共和国定位在国际因子空间中。这种解释仍保持描述性,并依赖于国家监测系统的质量。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过突出刚果民主共和国与其他国家相比的流行病学特征,为监测策略和猴痘风险评估提供信息。这种方法可能支持有针对性的公共卫生干预措施和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
证据基于摘要的仅限审查,研究未提供详细的统计或方法学验证。

ANALYSE MULTIVARIÉE DES PROFILS ÉPIDÉMIOLOGIQUES DE LA MPOX À L’ÉCHELLE INTERNATIONALE PAR ANALYSE EN COMPOSANTES PRINCIPALES : Positionnement de la République Démocratique du Congo

Authors: Sonia Arlette Nsenia Mpasi, Rostin Matendo Mabela, Jean Otshudi Otshudiakoy
Original abstract (English)
This paper presents a descriptive multivariate analysis of international mpox epidemiological profiles using principal component analysis (PCA). The dataset was obtained from the Our World in Data mpox database, derived from World Health Organization data, and covers the period from 1 May 2022 to 28 February 2026. After removing regional and global aggregates, checking ISO codes, handling missing values, and constructing country-level indicators, 144 countries and territories were retained. Fifteen variables describing cumulative burden, mortality, peak intensity, temporal dispersion, and duration of circulation were analyzed. Bartlett’s test of sphericity (χ2 = 2972.55; p < 0.001) and the overall KMO index (0.668) supported the use of exploratory PCA. The first three components explained 71.55% of the total variance. The first component mainly represented observed intensity and severity, the second duration and persistence of circulation, and the third the contrast between case burden and death burden. The applied contribution of the study lies in transforming daily surveillance series into a reproducible country-by-indicator matrix and positioning the Democratic Republic of the Congo within the international factorial space. The Democratic Republic of the Congo had a high PC1 score and a positive PC2 score, consistent with a profile of high reported burden and sustained circulation. This interpretation remains descriptive and depends on the quality of national surveillance systems.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This paper presents a descriptive multivariate analysis of international mpox epidemiological profiles using principal component analysis (PCA). The study aims to position the Democratic Republic of the Congo within the international factorial space of mpox disease patterns.
Design and population
The dataset was obtained from the Our World in Data mpox database, derived from World Health Organization data, and covers the period from 1 May 2022 to 28 February 2026. After removing regional and global aggregates, checking ISO codes, handling missing values, and constructing country-level indicators, 144 countries and territories were retained.
Core methods
Fifteen variables describing cumulative burden, mortality, peak intensity, temporal dispersion, and duration of circulation were analyzed.
Main results
The first three components explained 71.55% of the total variance. The Democratic Republic of the Congo had a high PC1 score and a positive PC2 score, consistent with a profile of high reported burden and sustained circulation.
Interpretation and novelty
The applied contribution of the study lies in transforming daily surveillance series into a reproducible country-by-indicator matrix and positioning the Democratic Republic of the Congo within the international factorial space. This interpretation remains descriptive and depends on the quality of national surveillance systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies and risk assessment for mpox by highlighting the epidemiological profile of the Democratic Republic of the Congo relative to other countries. This approach may support targeted public health interventions and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an abstract-only review, and the study does not provide detailed statistical or methodological validation.

北卡罗来纳州,美国,2023年10月–2025年5月污水中发现的猴痘病毒监测

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025
作者： Nicole L. Snyder, Thomas Clerkin, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Mara B. Tate, Ty L. Lautenschlager, Justin Albertson, Emma L. Doran, Erica F. Wilson, Rachel T. Noble, Virginia T. Guidry
摘要中文翻译我们使用了一个+17天的窗口期分析了废水检测(n = 47)和临床病例(n = 45)。早期研究表明,病毒通过尿液、粪便、呼吸道分泌物和皮肤病变传播,可以在废水中检测到猴痘病毒(MPXV),支持废水为基础的监测作为监测和量化社区猴痘病毒(MPXV)传播的有效方法(2,7,8)。在一项研究中,废水中的MPXV检测被发现早于临床病例的识别,突显其在早期检测中的潜力(9)。另一项研究报告称,在临床病例报告之前,废水中的病毒浓度有所上升(5)。为了评估北卡罗来纳州废水监测计划的有效性,我们进行了这项研究,以估计相对于确认的临床病例的敏感性、特异性、阳性预测值(PPV)和阴性预测值(NPV)。研究区域和监测时间:我们进行了回顾性诊断性能分析,以评估废水监测在北卡罗来纳州检测MPXV传播的效果。研究包括16个公共废水处理厂(WWTPs)的数据,这些处理厂服务的总人口约为2,002,864人(表1)。免责声明:早期发布文章不被视为最终版本。任何更改将在文章正式发布当月的在线版本中反映。第32卷,增刊——2026年夏季 增刊:北卡罗来纳州美国猴痘病毒废水监测,2023年10月至2025年5月 引用本文:媒体摘要引用 在2023年9月,北卡罗来纳州卫生与公共服务部在上次报告5个月后识别出一个新的猴痘病毒(MPXV)II类病例。为了评估可能未被识别的...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估废水监测在北卡罗来纳州美国检测猴痘病毒(MPXV)传播的有效性,鉴于自该病毒在非流行地区出现以来,其公共卫生关注程度不断上升。
设计与对象
The study was conducted across 16 wastewater treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents in North Carolina from October 2023 to May 2025. A total of 801 samples were collected during this period.
核心方法
使用CDC非正痘病毒通用实时PCR检测法对MPXV DNA进行定量。该检测在废水基质中进行了优化,测试了从62°C到52°C的热梯度。基于泊松分布原理,理论检测限(LOD)估计为500 copies/L。确认的猴痘病毒(MPXV)病例定义为2022年州和地方流行病学家委员会的病例定义。
主要结果
总体而言,17个废水样本被归类为阳性,当限制在高于LOD的浓度时,敏感性从10.6%(95% CI 6.5%–16.7%)下降到1.4%(95% CI 0.4%–5.0%)。特异性在同一限制下从95.1%(95% CI 93.2%–96.5%)上升到97.7%(95% CI 96.3%–98.6%)。假阳性数量从32减少到15,而假阴性数量从127增加到140。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,废水为基础的监测可以为公共卫生响应提供补充信息,但其对单个病例的低敏感性表明其在早期预警能力方面可能存在局限。该研究强调了在时间上与报告病例一致时考虑微量检测的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果支持将废水监测作为监测社区猴痘病毒(MPXV)传播趋势的工具,特别是在低传播率的环境中,同时强调需要结合临床监测以提高检测准确性。
局限与证据强度
本研究承认临床病例监测作为参考标准是不完美的,无法捕捉所有感染。低敏感性和变化的阳性预测值(PPV)表明废水检测可能并不总是反映真实的感染状态,因此证据强度中等,由于这些局限性。

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025

Authors: Nicole L. Snyder, Thomas Clerkin, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Mara B. Tate, Ty L. Lautenschlager, Justin Albertson, Emma L. Doran, Erica F. Wilson, Rachel T. Noble, Virginia T. Guidry
Original abstract (English)
We analyzed wastewater detections (n = 47) and clinical cases (n = 45) using a +17-day window. Early work demonstrated that viral shedding through urine, feces, respiratory secretions, and skin lesions can result in detectable MPXV in wastewater, supporting wastewater-based epidemiology as a viable approach for monitoring and quantifying community transmission (2,7,8). In 1 study, MPXV detections in wastewater were shown to precede the identification of the clinical cases, highlighting its potential for early outbreak detection (9). Another study reported increases in wastewater viral concentrations ahead of reported clinical cases (5). To evaluate mpox wastewater surveillance program in North Carolina, we conducted this study to estimate sensitivity, specificity, positive predictive value (PPV), and negative predictive value (NPV) relative to confirmed clinical cases. Study Area and Surveillance Period We conducted a retrospective diagnostic performance analysis to evaluate wastewater surveillance for detecting MPXV circulation in North Carolina. The study included data from 16 publicly owned wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) with defined catchment areas serving a combined population of 2,002,864 persons (Table 1). View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of wastewater surveillance for detecting mpox virus in North Carolina, USA, given the increasing public health concern associated with mpox since its emergence in a nonendemic region.
Design and population
The study was conducted across 16 wastewater treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents in North Carolina from October 2023 to May 2025. A total of 801 samples were collected during this period.
Core methods
MPXV DNA was quantified using the CDC Non-variola Orthopoxvirus Generic Real-Time PCR Test. The assay was optimized in a wastewater matrix by testing thermal gradients from 62°C to 52°C. The limit of detection (LOD) was theoretically estimated at 500 copies/L based on Poisson distribution principles. A confirmed mpox case was defined by the Council of State and Territorial Epidemiologists case definition from 2022.
Main results
Overall, 17 wastewater samples were classified as positive, with sensitivity decreasing from 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%–16.7%) to 1.4% (95% CI 0.4%–5.0%) when restricted to concentrations above the LOD. Specificity increased from 95.1% (95% CI 93.2%–96.5%) to 97.7% (95% CI 96.3%–98.6%) under the same restriction. The number of false positives decreased from 32 to 15, while false negatives increased from 127 to 140.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that wastewater-based surveillance can provide complementary awareness for public health response, but its low sensitivity for detecting single cases indicates potential limitations in early warning capacity. The study highlights the importance of considering trace detections for temporal concordance with reported cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the use of wastewater surveillance as a tool for monitoring community transmission trends of mpox virus, particularly in low-incidence settings, while emphasizing the need for complementary clinical surveillance to improve detection accuracy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that clinical case surveillance is an imperfect reference standard and does not capture all infections. The low sensitivity and variable positive predictive value (PPV) suggest that wastewater detections may not always reflect true infection status, and the evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

HECA-YOLOv11:一种高精度混合注意力网络,用于实时猴痘皮肤病变检测并具有可解释性

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability
作者： Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
摘要中文翻译简介:全球 暴发 猴痘风险需要高效的诊断工具。目前的方法速度缓慢,并且难以区分相似的皮肤病变,而深度学习模型缺乏多类分类的准确性和可解释性。本研究旨在开发一种高性能、实时筛查工具,用于早期检测猴痘和类似病症。方法:本研究提出了一种增强的架构,将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制纳入 YOLOv11 中,以改进特征选择。HECA 对输入特征图执行全局平均池化 (GAP) 和最大平均池化 (MAP)。该研究利用空间变换和像素域增强,建立并增强了包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的四类数据集。梯度加权类激活映射 (Grad-CAM) 生成用于病变定位可视化的热图,而基于 PyQt5 的交互式诊断界面则简化了临床工作流程。结果:实验结果表明,与测试集上的现有方法(EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201 和 YOLOv11s)相比,所提出的模型具有优越的性能,具有增强的检测速度、准确度(98.9%)、精确度(99%)、召回率(99.3%)和 F1 分数(99.1%)。在所有评估指标中,HECA-YOLOv11 的性能均优于 SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11 超过 5.1%。热图分析证实了该模型对病变区域的关注,与医学推理相一致。讨论:集成的注意力机制和可解释性技术有效地解决了自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制。高性能和低延迟标志着可靠临床决策支持的重大进步,对早期 暴发 遏制具有重要意义。结论:HECA-YOLOv11框架为早期猴痘筛查提供了高度准确和高效的工具。其标准化界面在加速公共卫生应对和改善新发传染病的全球控制方面具有巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生对快速、准确的诊断工具的迫切需求,以减轻 猴痘 的全球 暴发 风险,强调现有方法速度缓慢,而且深度学习模型缺乏多类皮肤病变分类的准确性和可解释性。
设计与对象
作者开发并评估了一个深度学习框架 (HECA-YOLOv11),旨在使用四类图像数据集实时筛查 猴痘 和类似的皮肤病变。
核心方法
该架构将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制集成到YOLOv11中,将全局平均池化和最大平均池化应用于输入特征图,并使用空间变换和像素域增强来增强包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的数据集。
主要结果
在测试集上,HECA-YOLOv11 实现了 98.9% 的准确率、99% 的精确率、99.3% 的召回率和 99.1% 的 F1 分数,优于 EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201、YOLOv11s、SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11所有指标均为 5.1%。
解释与创新
作者将集成注意力机制和可解释性技术解释为有效解决自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制,标志着早期 猴痘 筛查的可靠临床决策支持的重大进步。
科研与公卫意义
HECA-YOLOv11 的标准化界面和高性能表明有可能通过早期检测加速公共卫生反应并改善对新发传染病的全球控制。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告数据集规模、地理或人群范围、外部验证或前瞻性临床评估,限制了结论的普遍性,且证据强度为中等。

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability

Authors: Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: The global outbreak risk of monkeypox necessitates efficient diagnostic tools. Current methods are slow and struggle to differentiate similar skin lesions, while deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class classification. This study aims to develop a high-performance, real-time screening tool for early detection of monkeypox and similar conditions. Methods: This study proposes an enhanced architecture that incorporates the Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11 to improve feature selection. HECA performs Global Average Pooling (GAP) and Max Average Pooling (MAP) on input feature maps. Using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements, the research establishes and augments a four-class dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images. Gradient-Weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) generates heatmaps for lesion localization visualization, while a PyQt5-based interactive diagnostic interface streamlines clinical workflows. Results: Experimental results demonstrate the proposed model’s superior performance compared to existing approaches (EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, and YOLOv11s) on the test set, with enhanced detection speed, accuracy (98.9%), precision (99%), recall (99.3%), and F1-score (99.1%). HECA-YOLOv11 outperforms SE-YOLOv11 and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all evaluation metrics. Heatmap analysis confirms the model’s focus on lesion regions, aligning with medical reasoning. Discussion: The integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques effectively address key limitations in automated skin lesion detection. The high performance and low latency signify a major advancement towards reliable clinical decision support, with important implications for early outbreak containment. Conclusion: The HECA-YOLOv11 framework provides a highly accurate and efficient tool for early monkeypox screening. Its standardized interface holds significant potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for rapid, accurate diagnostic tools to mitigate the global outbreak risk of mpox, highlighting that existing methods are slow and deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class skin lesion classification.
Design and population
The authors developed and evaluated a deep learning framework (HECA-YOLOv11) designed for real-time screening of mpox and similar skin lesions using a four-class image dataset.
Core methods
The proposed architecture integrates a Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11, applying Global Average Pooling and Max Average Pooling to input feature maps, and augments a dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements.
Main results
On the test set, HECA-YOLOv11 achieved 98.9% accuracy, 99% precision, 99.3% recall, and 99.1% F1-score, outperforming EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, YOLOv11s, SE-YOLOv11, and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all metrics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques as effectively addressing key limitations in automated skin lesion detection, marking a major advancement toward reliable clinical decision support for early mpox screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The standardized interface and high performance of HECA-YOLOv11 suggest potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases through early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report dataset size, geographic or demographic scope, external validation, or prospective clinical evaluation, limiting generalisability and leaving the strength of evidence moderate.

猴痘病毒(MPXV)对宿主细胞因子反应的特征及其限制能力研究

Characterisation of host cytokine responses to mpox infection and their restrictive capacity
作者： Hannah Elizabeth Ashby
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒(MPXV)是一种正痘病毒(正痘病毒)在2022年重新出现,导致全球范围内显著的发病率。猴痘疾病表现为特征性的皮肤病变和系统性炎症症状。目前对MPXV如何调节人类先天免疫和细胞因子反应了解甚少,尽管从其他正痘病毒中获得的知识表明其可能具有显著的逃逸能力。本研究显示,MPXV感染人类角质形成细胞会导致以IL-6、IL-8、IL-11和LIF升高为特征的炎症细胞因子反应,这种反应与其他正痘病毒不同。基因操作揭示了细胞因子诱导依赖于核酸感应和多种转录因子。尽管这些细胞因子激活了人类成纤维细胞并激活了STAT3,但并未限制病毒复制。相反,使用IL-1β、TNFα和IFNβ激活细胞则导致病毒焦点减少,并显著限制了MPXV和VACV的复制。此外,对细胞因子反应的比较分析表明,IL-1β和TNFα上调了一部分ISG蛋白组,并且IL-1β和TNFα的限制作用部分被JAK1/2抑制剂鲁索替尼缓解,表明ISG依赖的抗病毒药物活性。进一步分析表明,IFN的限制作用部分由跨膜蛋白家族IFITM介导,这与在建立感染水平上的限制一致。传播实验显示,IFITMs通过EEV机制抑制VACV的传播,并限制多种正痘病毒的病毒滴度。令人惊讶的是,我们的研究确定IFITM拮抗是正痘病毒感染的一个保守特征。总体而言,这项工作揭示了MPXV与人类先天反应之间演化出的独特相互作用,其特点是选择性调节炎症和趋化信号传导,同时有效抑制抗病毒药物细胞因子。此外,预激活细胞与IFN显示OPXV对限制性ISG的特异性拮抗,表明OPXV与ISG蛋白组的相互作用为IFN基抗病毒药物提供了新的抗病毒药物因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征宿主细胞因子对猴痘感染的反应,并研究其限制能力。猴痘病毒(MPXV)是一种正痘病毒(正痘病毒),在2022年重新出现,导致全球范围内显著的发病率。
设计与对象
本研究涉及MPXV感染人类角质形成细胞,并分析细胞因子反应。此外,还包括使用不同细胞因子激活人类成纤维细胞以评估其对病毒复制的影响。
核心方法
利用MPXV感染人类角质形成细胞来研究细胞因子反应,并采用基因操作来确定细胞因子诱导对核酸感应和转录因子的依赖性。研究还涉及使用细胞因子激活人类成纤维细胞,以评估其对病毒复制的影响。
主要结果
MPXV感染人类角质形成细胞导致以IL-6、IL-8、IL-11和LIF升高为特征的炎症细胞因子反应,这种反应与其他正痘病毒不同。使用IL-1β、TNFα和IFNβ激活细胞导致病毒焦点减少,并显著限制了MPXV和VACV的复制。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,MPXV已演化出与人类先天反应的独特相互作用,其特点是选择性调节炎症和趋化信号传导,同时有效抑制抗病毒药物细胞因子。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提供了关于细胞因子反应及其对猴痘病毒限制机制的见解,这可能有助于开发基于IFN的抗病毒药物,并提高对病毒发病机制的理解。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于体外实验,未考虑体内条件,这可能限制研究结果的普遍适用性。证据强度中等,因为其来源于一项研究,且生物背景有限。

Characterisation of host cytokine responses to mpox infection and their restrictive capacity

Authors: Hannah Elizabeth Ashby
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is an orthopoxvirus (OPXV) that re-emerged in 2022 causing significant morbidity worldwide. Mpox disease presents with characteristic skin lesions and systemic inflammatory symptoms. Little is known about how MPXV modulates human innate immunity and cytokine responses, although significant evasion is expected from knowledge obtained from other OPXV. In this study I show that MPXV infection of human keratinocytes results in an inflammatory cytokine response defined by elevated IL-6, IL-8, IL-11 and LIF that was not shared with other OPXV. Genetic manipulation revealed that cytokine induction is dependent on nucleic acid sensing and multiple transcription factors. Activation of human fibroblasts with these cytokines did not restrict viral replication despite activating STAT3. Conversely, activation with IL-1β, TNFα and IFNβ resulted in a reduction of viral foci and significant restriction of MPXV and VACV replication. Furthermore, comparative analysis of cytokine responses highlighted that IL-1β and TNFα upregulate a subset of the ISG proteome and restriction by IL-1β and TNFα was partially alleviated by JAK1/2 inhibitor ruxolitinib, indicating ISG-dependent antiviral activity. Further analysis highlighted that IFN restriction was partly mediated by the transmembrane protein family IFITM, consistent with restriction at the level of establishing infection. Spread assays revealed that IFITMs impair VACV spread, mediated by EEV and restrict viral titres of a range of OPXV. Surprisingly, our study determined that IFITM antagonism is a conserved feature of OPXV infection. Collectively, this work reveals that MPXV has evolved a distinctive interplay with human innate responses characterized by selective modulation of inflammatory and chemotactic signalling whilst retaining effective suppression of antiviral cytokines. Furthermore, preactivating cells with IFN revealed selective antagonism of restrictive ISG by OPXV, indicating that OPXV interactions with the ISG proteome provide a source of novel antiviral factors to be exploited for IFN-based antivirals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterise the host cytokine responses to mpox infection and investigate their restrictive capacity. Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is an orthopoxvirus (OPXV) that re-emerged in 2022 causing significant morbidity worldwide.
Design and population
The study involved the infection of human keratinocytes with MPXV and the analysis of cytokine responses. It also included activation of human fibroblasts with various cytokines to assess their effect on viral replication.
Core methods
MPXV infection of human keratinocytes was used to examine cytokine responses, and genetic manipulation was employed to determine the dependence of cytokine induction on nucleic acid sensing and transcription factors. The study also involved activation of human fibroblasts with cytokines to evaluate their impact on viral replication.
Main results
MPXV infection of human keratinocytes resulted in an inflammatory cytokine response defined by elevated IL-6, IL-8, IL-11 and LIF that was not shared with other OPXV. Activation with IL-1β, TNFα and IFNβ resulted in a reduction of viral foci and significant restriction of MPXV and VACV replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that MPXV has evolved a distinctive interplay with human innate responses, characterised by selective modulation of inflammatory and chemotactic signalling while retaining effective suppression of antiviral cytokines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the mechanisms of cytokine responses and their restrictive capacity against mpox virus, which could inform the development of IFN-based antivirals and improve understanding of viral pathogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro experiments and does not account for in vivo conditions, which may limit the generalisability of the findings. The evidence is moderate as it is derived from a single study with limited biological context.

免疫缺陷人群中历史天花疫苗接种后残余免疫应答及探索性暴发建模后的剩余免疫应答

Residual immune responses after historical smallpox vaccination and exploratory outbreak modeling in immunocompromised populations
作者： Xuetong Zhu, Jiayin Wang, Xuanzhi Wang, Zheyu Tu, Qi Zhou, Yilong Zhu, Jiancheng Xu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在常规天花疫苗接种停止以及猴痘 暴发出现后,针对正痘病毒的免疫力再次受到关注。本研究对具有历史天花疫苗接种记录的个体中免疫应答进行了特征描述。
设计与对象
基于已发表的流行病学参数,采用探索性数学模型评估了免疫缺陷人群中假设的传播情景。分析包括112份外周血单核细胞和3,202份血清样本。
核心方法
通过酶联免疫吸附试验和酶联免疫斑点试验评估了历史天花疫苗接种后细胞和体液中的免疫应答。利用回归分析评估了临床特征、实验室指标与免疫应答之间的关联。
主要结果
The proportion of individuals with negative neutralizing antibody responses decreased with age but was higher among disease groups than in the healthy group. Chronic disease (odds ratio [OR], 0.67; 95% CI, 0.44–0.98) and cancer (OR, 0.58; 95% CI, 0.37–0.90) were associated with lower odds of high antibody positivity. High-density lipoprotein cholesterol and aspartate aminotransferase levels were positively associated with antibody levels, whereas cholinesterase showed an inverse association.
解释与创新
具有历史天花疫苗接种记录的个体显示出可检测的残余免疫应答,不同合并症组之间存在差异,并且与实验室指标相关。在假设情景下的探索性建模表明,免疫缺陷人群中模拟感染和死亡率模式存在差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来的正痘病毒应对和预防策略提供信息。免疫缺陷个体可能需要进一步评估其对潜在正痘病毒的易感性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究设计或统计分析的具体局限性。由于建模的探索性质和对历史疫苗接种数据的关注,证据强度中等。

Residual immune responses after historical smallpox vaccination and exploratory outbreak modeling in immunocompromised populations

Authors: Xuetong Zhu, Jiayin Wang, Xuanzhi Wang, Zheyu Tu, Qi Zhou, Yilong Zhu, Jiancheng Xu
Original abstract (English)
Following the discontinuation of routine smallpox vaccination and the emergence of mpox outbreaks, immunity against orthopoxviruses has received renewed attention. This study characterized immune responses among individuals with historical smallpox vaccination. Separately, an exploratory mathematical model was used to assess hypothetical transmission scenarios among immunocompromised populations. Cellular and humoral immune responses following historical smallpox vaccination were assessed using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay and enzyme-linked immunospot assay. Associations between clinical characteristics, laboratory markers, and immune responses were evaluated using regression analyses. An exploratory susceptible-infected-recovered-dead model based on published epidemiological parameters was used to assess hypothetical transmission scenarios among immunocompromised populations. Analysis included 112 peripheral blood mononuclear cell and 3,202 serum samples. Cellular immune responses were limited among individuals with historical smallpox vaccination, with no significant differences observed between healthy and disease groups. Vaccinated individuals with comorbidities had lower vaccinia virus Tianan strain-specific immunoglobulin G titers than the healthy group ( P < 0.001), with variations observed across chronic disease subgroups. The proportion of individuals with negative neutralizing antibody responses decreased with age but was higher among disease groups than in the healthy group. Chronic disease (odds ratio [OR], 0.67; 95% CI, 0.44–0.98) and cancer (OR, 0.58; 95% CI, 0.37–0.90) were associated with lower odds of high antibody positivity. High-density lipoprotein cholesterol and aspartate aminotransferase levels were positively associated with antibody levels, whereas cholinesterase showed an inverse association. Exploratory modeling under hypothetical scenarios suggested differences in simulated infection and mortality patterns among immunocompromised populations, with higher simulated infection proportions in individuals aged 15–19 years and higher mortality proportions in those aged ≥ 70 years. Individuals with historical smallpox vaccination showed detectable residual immune responses, with variations observed across comorbidity groups and in association with laboratory markers. Immunocompromised individuals may warrant further evaluation for potential orthopoxvirus susceptibility. These findings may inform future orthopoxvirus preparedness and prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Exploratory modeling under hypothetical scenarios suggested differences in simulated infection and mortality patterns among immunocompromised populations.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or statistical analyses. The evidence strength is moderate due to the exploratory nature of the modeling and the focus on historical vaccination data.

猴痘病毒 传播动力学的稳定性和灵敏度分析

Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus
作者： D.A. Arhinful, A. Y. Anafo, I. Ampofi, A.Y. Anafo, Ampofi I.
摘要中文翻译基本再生数$R_{0}$是使用下一代矩阵方法导出的。这些结果为设计有针对性的 猴痘 控制干预措施提供了定量基础,特别是通过减少接触和提高 治疗 覆盖范围。猴痘病毒 D.A.的传播动力学稳定性和灵敏度分析Ampofi 矿业技术大学数学科学系,加纳塔夸 通讯作者:[email protected] 文章信息:期刊:International Journal of Advances in Applied Mathematics and Mechanics (IJAAMM) Volume: 14,Issue: 1,Pages: 108-121 Issue Date: September 2026 License: CC BY 4.0 DOI: https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 文章历史:2026-06-22 00:00:00 接收,2026-08-14 00:00:00 修订,2026-08-25 00:00:00 接受,2026 年 8 月 27 日在线发布引用:D.A. Ampofi,传播 动力学的稳定性和灵敏度分析,Int。14(1)(2026)108-121。https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 摘要 针对人类和啮齿类动物群体相互作用中的 猴痘 传播 动态制定了确定性区室模型。人群分为易感人群、暴露人群、传染人群、隔离人群和康复人群;将啮齿类动物分为易感、暴露和传染性类别。纳入了隔离至易感人群的途径,以捕获重新进入高危人群的未发现病例。当 $R_{0}<1$ 时,无病平衡的局部渐近稳定性成立,并且使用 Castillo-Chavez 和 Song 方法证明了全局渐近稳定性。还导出了地方性平衡的劳斯-赫尔维茨条件。归一化前向敏感性分析确定了人与人之间暴露的比率和比例...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过稳定性和敏感性分析来分析 传播 的动态,以了解影响其传播的因素并为控制策略提供信息。
设计与对象
建立了一个确定性分层模型来研究猴痘在人类和啮齿类动物种群中的动态变化,模型中包含了人类的易感、暴露、感染、隔离和康复等类别,以及啮齿类动物的易感、暴露和感染等类别。
核心方法
本研究采用了一个确定性分层模型,包括人类的易感、暴露、感染、隔离和康复等状态,以及啮齿类动物的易感、暴露和感染等状态。基本再生数 $R_0$ 通过下一代矩阵方法计算,平衡点的稳定性则通过Castillo-Chavez和Song的方法以及Routh-Hurwitz条件进行分析。
主要结果
研究结果表明,当 $R_0 < 1$ 时,无病平衡点是局部渐近稳定的,且在相同条件下达到全局渐近稳定。数值模拟进一步证实,当 $R_0 < 1$ 时,所有感染状态都会渐近地衰减至零,表明疾病可以被消除。
解释与创新
作者认为,人类之间的传播传播率以及暴露人类进入感染类别的比例是猴痘传播的主要驱动因素。他们还指出,提高康复率和治疗率可以降低 $R_0$,为有针对性的干预措施提供了定量依据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为设计针对性的猴痘控制干预措施提供了定量框架,特别是通过减少接触率和提高治疗覆盖率来降低基本再生数。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于一个确定性模型,假设传播动态变化中没有随机性。其证据强度较高,因为使用了数学方法和模拟来支持结论,但模型的假设可能限制了其在现实场景中的适用性。

Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus

Authors: D.A. Arhinful, A. Y. Anafo, I. Ampofi, A.Y. Anafo, Ampofi I.
Original abstract (English)
The basic reproduction number $R_{0}$ is derived using the next-generation matrix method. These results offer a quantitative basis for designing targeted Mpox control interventions, particularly through contact reduction and improved treatment coverage. Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus D.A. Ampofi Department of Mathematical Sciences, University of Mines and Technology, Tarkwa, Ghana Corresponding Author: [email protected] Article Information: Journal: International Journal of Advances in Applied Mathematics and Mechanics (IJAAMM) Volume: 14, Issue: 1, Pages: 108-121 Issue Date: September 2026 License: CC BY 4.0 DOI: https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 Article History: Received 2026-06-22 00:00:00, Revised 2026-08-14 00:00:00, Accepted 2026-08-25 00:00:00, Published online 27 August 2026 Cite: D.A. Ampofi , Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus, Int. 14(1) (2026) 108-121. https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 Abstract A deterministic compartmental model is formulated for Mpox transmission dynamics in interacting human and rodent populations. The human population is divided into susceptible, exposed, infectious, quarantined, and recovered classes; the rodent population into susceptible, exposed, and infectious classes. A quarantined-to-susceptible pathway is incorporated to capture undetected cases re-entering the at-risk population. Local asymptotic stability of the disease-free equilibrium is established whenever $R_{0}<1$, and global asymptotic stability is proved using the Castillo-Chavez and Song approach. Routh-Hurwitz conditions are also derived for the endemic equilibrium. Normalised forward sensitivity analysis identifies the human-to-human rate and proportion of exposed h...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to analyze the transmission dynamics of Mpox virus through stability and sensitivity analysis to understand the factors influencing its spread and inform control strategies.
Design and population
A deterministic compartmental model was formulated to study Mpox transmission dynamics in both human and rodent populations, incorporating classes such as susceptible, exposed, infectious, quarantined, and recovered for humans, and susceptible, exposed, and infectious for rodents.
Core methods
The study used a deterministic compartmental model with compartments for susceptible, exposed, infectious, quarantined, and recovered human populations, and susceptible, exposed, and infectious rodent populations. The basic reproduction number $R_0$ was calculated using the next-generation matrix method, and stability of equilibria was analyzed using Castillo-Chavez and Song approach as well as Routh-Hurwitz conditions.
Main results
The results showed that the disease-free equilibrium is locally asymptotically stable when $R_0 < 1$, and global asymptotic stability is achieved under the same condition. Numerical simulations confirmed that all infectious compartments decline asymptotically to zero when $R_0 < 1$, indicating disease elimination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the human-to-human transmission rate and the proportion of exposed humans moving to the infectious class are the dominant drivers of Mpox transmission. They also noted that increasing recovery and treatment rates can reduce $R_0$, offering a quantitative basis for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a quantitative framework for designing targeted Mpox control interventions, particularly by focusing on reducing contact rates and improving treatment coverage to lower the basic reproduction number.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a deterministic model, which assumes no randomness in transmission dynamics. The evidence is strong as it uses mathematical methods and simulations to support its conclusions, but the model's assumptions may limit its applicability to real-world scenarios.

使用复合采集系统和优化的RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a检测病毒生物气溶胶的高灵敏度分子检测

High-Sensitivity Molecular Detection of Viral Bioaerosols Using a Composite Collection System and Optimized RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a.
作者： Hai Fu, Zhigang Wang, Jianxin Guo, Tengfei Wang, Xiaoyi Li, Yimin Su, Zhi Chen, Huifeng Yang, Zhongjian Xie, Defa Li
摘要中文翻译高度致病的空气传播微生物对公共卫生构成重大威胁,特别是由于其微小的颗粒尺寸可以支持长时间悬浮和远距离传播。本文提出了一种集成平台,结合复合生物气溶胶采集系统、三阶段富集机制和重组酶聚合酶扩增-CRISPR/Cas12a(RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a)检测技术。该平台集成了基于模型的恒温控制、堆叠光学滤波器和多通道荧光成像系统。温度控制模型准确描述了反应模块的热响应,而光学和成像子系统支持稳定的荧光读取。使用气溶胶化的猴痘病毒(MPXV)假病毒作为受控测试目标,在测试条件下,该平台在采集液中检测到目标核酸的最低浓度为10 copies/μL。这些结果支持了在紧凑原型中整合气溶胶富集与等温分子检测的可行性。主要贡献在于生物气溶胶采集和富集的创新及工程整合、分子检测中生化反应的恒温控制以及高精度荧光检测技术。关于分子检测方法,生化反应参数是通过实验优化的,而不是引入新的生化反应机制。在做出诊断或现场应用声明之前,仍需使用真实的临床或环境病毒气溶胶进行验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一种对空气传播病毒病原体具有高灵敏度的分子检测方法,以应对其微小颗粒尺寸所带来的公共卫生威胁,以及其支持长时间悬浮和远距离传播的能力。
设计与对象
本研究展示了一种集成平台,该平台结合了复合生物气溶胶采集系统、三阶段富集机制和RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a检测技术,使用气溶胶化的猴痘病毒假病毒作为受控测试目标。
核心方法
该平台集成了基于模型的恒温控制、堆叠光学滤波器和多通道荧光成像系统,生化反应参数通过实验优化,而非引入新的反应机制。
主要结果
在测试条件下,该平台在采集液中检测到目标核酸的最低浓度为10 copies/μL,温度控制模型准确描述了反应模块的热响应,光学和成像子系统支持稳定的荧光读取。
解释与创新
这些结果支持了在紧凑原型中整合气溶胶富集与等温分子检测的可行性,突出了生物气溶胶采集、热控制和荧光检测技术方面的创新。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发更有效的监测和诊断工具提供了信息,特别是在需要快速和灵敏检测的环境中。
局限与证据强度
本研究使用了气溶胶化的假病毒作为测试目标,仍需使用真实的临床或环境病毒气溶胶进行验证,这限制了其在实际应用中的证据强度。

High-Sensitivity Molecular Detection of Viral Bioaerosols Using a Composite Collection System and Optimized RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a.

Authors: Hai Fu, Zhigang Wang, Jianxin Guo, Tengfei Wang, Xiaoyi Li, Yimin Su, Zhi Chen, Huifeng Yang, Zhongjian Xie, Defa Li
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic airborne microorganisms pose a significant public health threat, particularly because their small particle size can support prolonged suspension and long-range transport. Here, we present an integrated platform that combines a composite bioaerosol collection system with a three-stage enrichment mechanism and recombinase polymerase amplification-CRISPR/Cas12a (RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a) detection. The platform incorporates model-based constant-temperature control, stacked optical filters, and multichannel fluorescence imaging. The temperature-control model accurately described the thermal response of the reaction module, while the optical and imaging subsystems supported stable fluorescence readout. Using aerosolized monkeypox virus (MPXV) pseudovirus as a controlled test target, the platform detected target nucleic acid in collection liquid down to 10 copies/μL under the tested conditions. These results support the feasibility of integrating aerosol enrichment with isothermal molecular detection in a compact prototype. The main contributions lie in innovations and the engineering integration of bioaerosol collection and enrichment, thermal control of biochemical reactions in molecular detection, and precision fluorescence detection techniques for molecular detection. Regarding molecular detection methods, the biochemical reaction parameters were experimentally optimized rather than introducing new biochemical reaction mechanisms. Validation with authentic clinical or environmental viral aerosols remains necessary before diagnostic or field-use claims can be made.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a highly sensitive molecular detection method for airborne viral pathogens, addressing the public health threat posed by their small particle size and ability to support prolonged suspension and long-range transport.
Design and population
The study presented an integrated platform combining a composite bioaerosol collection system with a three-stage enrichment mechanism and RPA-CRISPR/Cas12a detection, using aerosolized monkeypox virus pseudovirus as a controlled test target.
Core methods
The platform incorporated model-based constant-temperature control, stacked optical filters, and multichannel fluorescence imaging, with biochemical reaction parameters experimentally optimized rather than introducing new mechanisms.
Main results
The platform detected target nucleic acid in collection liquid down to 10 copies/μL under tested conditions, with the temperature-control model accurately describing the thermal response of the reaction module and the optical and imaging subsystems supporting stable fluorescence readout.
Interpretation and novelty
The results support the feasibility of integrating aerosol enrichment with isothermal molecular detection in a compact prototype, highlighting innovations in bioaerosol collection, thermal control, and fluorescence detection techniques.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more effective surveillance and diagnostic tools for airborne viral pathogens, particularly in settings where rapid and sensitive detection is critical.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used aerosolized pseudovirus as a test target, and validation with authentic clinical or environmental viral aerosols remains necessary, which limits the strength of the evidence for real-world application.

商业大型语言模型对肛周皮肤病变图像的诊断准确性:对Gemini、Claude和ChatGPT的比较研究。

Diagnostic Accuracy of Commercial Large Language Models for Anogenital Skin Lesion Images: A Comparative Study of Gemini, Claude, and ChatGPT.
作者： Nyi Nyi Soe, Phyu Mon Latt, David Lee, Ei T Aung, Ryan Horn, Jason J Ong, Christopher K Fairley, Eric P F Chow
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肛周皮肤病的诊断通常需要专科专业知识,而这种专业知识在许多临床环境中并不容易获得。大型语言模型(LLMs)正变得越来越容易被临床医生使用,但它们在肛周皮肤病诊断中的准确性尚未得到评估。
设计与对象
本研究于2025年9月至11月期间进行,使用了来自STI Atlas(stiatlas.org)和其他公开来源的去标识化肛周疾病临床图像。
核心方法
主要结果是正确分类图像(识别为性传播感染[STI]与非STI)以及LLMs在最可能的(top-1)、top-3或top-5鉴别诊断中包含正确的诊断。
主要结果
Among 218 images, Gemini achieved the highest accuracy for STI binary classification (76.2% [95% CI, 70.5%-81.9%]) and differential diagnosis (top-1, 39.0% [95% CI, 32.7%-45.7%]; top-3, 54.6% [95% CI, 47.9%-61.1%]; top-5, 60.6% [95% CI, 53.9%-66.9%]), followed by ChatGPT and Claude. In subgroup analysis, all LLMs showed substantially reduced accuracy for diagnostically challenging images (top-5 accuracy range, 29.2%-40.0%). None of the LLMs could identify any mpox correctly.
解释与创新
LLMs在肛周皮肤病诊断中的准确性有限,尤其是在具有挑战性的图像中。表现最好的模型(Gemini)在top-1 诊断中的准确性仅为39.0%,表明当前LLMs无法可靠地诊断肛周疾病。
科研与公卫意义
这些工具可能支持监督下的临床分诊,但在常规临床使用前需要进一步验证。未来的研究应将LLMs与临床医生进行比较,并探讨它们如何辅助临床诊断。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未具体报告与猴痘病毒相关的局限性。研究结果表明,LLMs在肛周疾病诊断中的准确性具有中等信心,但证据仅限于STI的一般类别,并未聚焦于猴痘。

Diagnostic Accuracy of Commercial Large Language Models for Anogenital Skin Lesion Images: A Comparative Study of Gemini, Claude, and ChatGPT.

Authors: Nyi Nyi Soe, Phyu Mon Latt, David Lee, Ei T Aung, Ryan Horn, Jason J Ong, Christopher K Fairley, Eric P F Chow
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Diagnosing anogenital dermatological conditions often requires specialist expertise that is unavailable in many clinical settings. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly accessible to clinicians, but their diagnostic accuracy for anogenital dermatology has not been evaluated. We evaluated the diagnostic accuracy of 3 LLMs (Gemini 2.5 Pro, Claude Opus 4.1, and ChatGPT 5 Thinking) for anogenital dermatological conditions. METHODS: This study was conducted between September and November 2025, using deidentified clinical images of anogenital conditions from the STI Atlas (stiatlas.org) and other publicly available sources. Primary outcomes were correct classification of images identified as sexually transmitted infections (STIs) vs non-STIs and the inclusion of the correct diagnosis among the LLMs' top-ranked (top-1), top-3, or top-5 differential diagnoses. RESULTS: Among 218 images, Gemini achieved the highest accuracy for STI binary classification (76.2% [95% CI, 70.5%-81.9%]) and differential diagnosis (top-1, 39.0% [95% CI, 32.7%-45.7%]; top-3, 54.6% [95% CI, 47.9%-61.1%]; top-5, 60.6% [95% CI, 53.9%-66.9%]), followed by ChatGPT and Claude. In subgroup analysis, all LLMs showed substantially reduced accuracy for diagnostically challenging images (top-5 accuracy range, 29.2%-40.0%). Gemini consistently outperformed Claude across most subgroups (P < .05). None of the LLMs could identify any mpox correctly. CONCLUSIONS: LLMs showed limited accuracy for diagnosing anogenital dermatological conditions, particularly for challenging images. The best-performing model (Gemini) achieved only 39.0% for top-1 diagnosis, indicating that current LLMs cannot reliably diagnose anogenital conditions. These tools may support supervised clinical triage but need further validation before routine clinical use. Future studies should compare LLMs with clinicians and explore how they can assist clinical diagnosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Diagnosing anogenital dermatological conditions often requires specialist expertise that is unavailable in many clinical settings. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly accessible to clinicians, but their diagnostic accuracy for anogenital dermatology has not been evaluated.
Design and population
This study was conducted between September and November 2025, using deidentified clinical images of anogenital conditions from the STI Atlas (stiatlas.org) and other publicly available sources.
Core methods
Primary outcomes were correct classification of images identified as sexually transmitted infections (STIs) vs non-STIs and the inclusion of the correct diagnosis among the LLMs' top-ranked (top-1), top-3, or top-5 differential diagnoses.
Main results
Among 218 images, Gemini achieved the highest accuracy for STI binary classification (76.2% [95% CI, 70.5%-81.9%]) and differential diagnosis (top-1, 39.0% [95% CI, 32.7%-45.7%]; top-3, 54.6% [95% CI, 47.9%-61.1%]; top-5, 60.6% [95% CI, 53.9%-66.9%]), followed by ChatGPT and Claude. In subgroup analysis, all LLMs showed substantially reduced accuracy for diagnostically challenging images (top-5 accuracy range, 29.2%-40.0%). None of the LLMs could identify any mpox correctly.
Interpretation and novelty
LLMs showed limited accuracy for diagnosing anogenital dermatological conditions, particularly for challenging images. The best-performing model (Gemini) achieved only 39.0% for top-1 diagnosis, indicating that current LLMs cannot reliably diagnose anogenital conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These tools may support supervised clinical triage but need further validation before routine clinical use. Future studies should compare LLMs with clinicians and explore how they can assist clinical diagnosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations related to the mpox virus specifically. The study's results suggest moderate confidence in the diagnostic accuracy of LLMs for anogenital conditions, but the evidence is limited to the general category of STIs and does not focus on mpox.

协调许可与推荐使用以加快疫苗在中非持续爆发的邦迪布古埃博拉病毒疫情中的获取

Aligning Licensure and Recommended Use to Expedite Vaccine Access in Response to the Ongoing Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Epidemic in Central Africa
作者： G. O’Hara, Sam Halabi
摘要中文翻译持续爆发的正埃博拉病毒邦迪布古亚种(Bundibugyo virus,BDBV)疫情是十年来最严重的埃博拉病毒暴发疫情,且迅速扩散,仅在刚果民主共和国(DRC)就已出现超过4600例疑似病例。该疫情已导致2186人死亡,并传播至邻国。由于BDBV尚无获批的疫苗,及时应对将需要采用针对其他埃博拉病毒获批的疫苗,并加快开发针对BDBV的新疫苗。获取疫苗对于公共卫生紧急响应至关重要,通常依赖于每个国家的两个专家机构:国家药品监管机构(例如美国食品药品监督管理局(FDA))和国家免疫顾问。传统全球卫生援助的减少加剧了DRC及其他面临近期埃博拉、马尔堡和猴痘疫情的低收入国家在获得疫苗方面的有限许可和推荐使用,从而扩大了高危人群获取的差距。因此,本文评论强调了强大的国家免疫顾问与扩大许可协调的重要性,以加快疫苗及其他关键疫情应对工具的获取。通过分析卢旺达和DRC的监管应对,本文展示了这两个机构之间职责明确划分对于有效应对的重要性,有助于优先考虑自给自足和更广泛的区域公共卫生合作,并从而实现对邦迪布古疫情的更快控制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在应对持续爆发的邦迪布古埃博拉病毒疫情所引发的迫切需求,以改善疫苗的获取,该疫情已对中非地区的公共卫生造成重大影响。
设计与对象
本研究是一项评论,分析刚果民主共和国(DRC)和卢旺达的监管与免疫框架,重点关注低收入国家在获取针对新兴暴发的疫苗过程中所面临的挑战。
核心方法
作者研究了国家药品监管机构和免疫顾问的作用,以刚果民主共和国和卢旺达为案例,突出明确划分职责对于疫苗获取和疫情应对的重要性。
主要结果
本研究指出,刚果民主共和国和卢旺达已出现超过4600例疑似病例和2186例死亡,疫情迅速扩散并影响邻国。
解释与创新
作者认为,由于BDBV尚无获批的疫苗,因此需要使用针对其他埃博拉病毒获批的疫苗,并开发新的疫苗,强调监管机构与免疫顾问之间协调行动的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了国家免疫顾问在加快疫苗获取和增强区域合作以控制暴发及与其他相关病毒方面的重要作用。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于摘要内容,未提供详细统计数据或方法学数据,因此其结论的强度仅限于中等水平。

Aligning Licensure and Recommended Use to Expedite Vaccine Access in Response to the Ongoing Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Epidemic in Central Africa

Authors: G. O’Hara, Sam Halabi
Original abstract (English)
The ongoing Orthoebolavirus bundibugyoense—Bundibugyo virus (BDBV)—epidemic is the worst Ebolavirus outbreak in a decade and is rapidly expanding, with >4600 suspected cases in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) alone. It has caused 2186 deaths and spread to neighboring countries. As BDBV has no approved vaccine, timely response will require adapted use of existing vaccines approved for other ebolaviruses and expedited development of novel vaccines targeted toward BDBV. Access to vaccines is fundamental to public health emergency response and generally depends on two expert bodies in each country: a national regulatory authority (e.g., the US Food and Drug Administration (FDA)) and a national immunization advisor. Receding traditional global health assistance compounds already-limited licensure and recommended use in the DRC and other low-income countries confronting recent outbreaks of Ebola, Marburg, and mpox, exacerbating access gaps for high-risk populations. This commentary thus clarifies the critical importance of a robust national immunization advisor, coordinated closely with expanded licensure, in expediting access to vaccines and other essential epidemic response tools. Using the regulatory responses of the Republic of Rwanda and DRC, it demonstrates that clearer delineation of roles between these bodies is critical for effective response, can help prioritize self-sufficiency and greater regional public health cooperation, and will thus enable swifter control of Bundibugyo.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent need for improved vaccine access in response to the ongoing Bundibugyo Ebolavirus epidemic, which has caused significant public health concerns in Central Africa.
Design and population
The study is a commentary analyzing the regulatory and immunization frameworks in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) and Rwanda, focusing on the challenges faced by low-income countries in accessing vaccines for emerging outbreaks.
Core methods
The authors examine the roles of national regulatory authorities and immunization advisors, using the DRC and Rwanda as case studies to highlight the importance of clear delineation of responsibilities in vaccine access and epidemic response.
Main results
The study reports that the DRC and Rwanda have experienced over 4600 suspected cases and 2186 deaths from the Bundibugyo virus, with the epidemic rapidly expanding and affecting neighboring countries.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the lack of an approved vaccine for BDBV necessitates the use of existing vaccines for other ebolaviruses and the development of novel ones, emphasizing the need for coordinated action between regulatory bodies and immunization advisors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the critical role of national immunization advisors in expediting vaccine access and enhancing regional cooperation to control outbreaks of mpox and other related viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence and does not provide detailed statistical or methodological data, limiting the strength of its conclusions to a moderate level.

通过社区主导、全球互联的传染病研究促进公平

Advancing equity through community-led, globally connected infectious disease research
作者： Pontiano Kaleebu, Aliya Naheed, Mahnaz Vahedi, Jackeline Alger, Lyda Osorio, Cristiani Vieira Machado, Francine Ntoumi, Mercedes Rumi, Mohammed Alkhaldi, Pacifique Ndishimye
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决全球传染病研究中的不平等现象,特别是突出像猴痘这样的疾病对低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)不成比例的影响,并强调需要更加公平的研究实践。
设计与对象
本研究涉及一个名为全球健康网络(TGHN)的社区主导、全球互联的研究平台,该平台在多个地区和国家运营,包括非洲、拉丁美洲和加勒比地区、南亚和东南亚以及中东和北非。它重点关注在LMICs中从事猴痘和其他传染病研究的团队。
核心方法
方法包括与全球倡议(如CEPI和PHEPREN)建立合作伙伴关系,组织多语言网络研讨会和研讨会,并促进知识交流以支持本地研究能力和独立性。
主要结果
TGHN平台成功举办了超过65个多语言网络研讨会和研讨会,涵盖各种传染病主题,包括猴痘,并将具有特定领域专业知识的研究团队(如妊娠队列研究研究)连接起来。它还支持了一项随机试验,证明使用广谱抗生素对结核病的诊断益处有限,这为抗菌药物管理实践提供了信息。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明协作的、社区主导的研究平台可以克服系统性障碍,使对传染病威胁(包括猴痘)的应对更加有效且由本地驱动。这种方法的新颖之处在于其强调将研究嵌入常规医疗保健服务中,并促进本地证据的产生。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,加强本地研究体系并促进合作伙伴关系可以提高对像猴痘这样的新兴传染病的检测和应对能力,从而推动更加公平和有效的公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
这些证据基于一项单一研究,并依赖于合作伙伴和网络研讨会报告的结果,这可能缺乏直接、针对性的实验数据。然而,该研究为社区主导研究在解决全球健康不平等问题中的重要性提供了有力的论据。

Advancing equity through community-led, globally connected infectious disease research

Authors: Pontiano Kaleebu, Aliya Naheed, Mahnaz Vahedi, Jackeline Alger, Lyda Osorio, Cristiani Vieira Machado, Francine Ntoumi, Mercedes Rumi, Mohammed Alkhaldi, Pacifique Ndishimye
Original abstract (English)
These include gaps in core research competencies, such as methods, data management, community engagement and communication, as well as ethics and integrity2. TGHN’s founding principle was to facilitate mobilization of research methods between researchers, and the platform is led through a franchise governance model with regional and country coordination. nature microbiology https://doi.org/10.1038/s41564-026-02489-y Comment Advancing equity through community-led, globally connected infectious disease research Pontiano Kaleebu, Aliya Naheed, Mahnaz Vahedi, Jackeline Alger, Lyda Osorio, Cristiani Vieira Machado, Francine Ntoumi, Mercedes Rumi, Mohammed AlKhaldi, Pacifique Ndishimye, Evelyn Gitau & Trudie Lang Platforms for community-led but globally connected knowledge and resource sharing can move us towards more equitable and more effective research and solutions for infectious diseases. The burden of infectious disease is vast and global, yet research capac- ity remains starkly unequal around the world. Most infectious disease research funding goes to high-income countries, where research agendas are set, studies led and benefits realized1. Meanwhile, major endemic diseases such as tuberculosis, malaria and cholera dispropor- tionately affect the poorest, least resourced communities, and newer or re-emerging threats such as Ebola, Zika and mpox occur in settings least equipped to respond. The COVID-19 pandemic underscored that research is the pathway out of a pandemic. This is just as true for tackling long-standing infec- tious diseases. An entire connected ecosystem encompassing different types of research is needed to make progress: basic and preclinical research to understand pathogens and host responses; observational studies to assess disease impact and guide pu...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the global inequity in infectious disease research, particularly highlighting the disproportionate impact of diseases like mpox on low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) and the need for more equitable research practices.
Design and population
The study involved a community-led, globally connected research platform called The Global Health Network (TGHN), which operates in multiple regions and countries, including Africa, Latin America and the Caribbean, South and Southeast Asia, and the Middle East and North Africa. It focused on research teams in LMICs, particularly those working on mpox and other infectious diseases.
Core methods
The methods included establishing partnerships with global initiatives such as CEPI and PHEPREN, organizing multilingual webinars and workshops, and facilitating knowledge exchange to support local research capacity and independence.
Main results
The TGHN platform successfully enabled over 65 multilingual webinars and workshops on various infectious disease topics, including mpox, and connected research teams with expertise in specific areas like pregnancy cohort studies. It also supported a randomized trial demonstrating the limited diagnostic benefit of using broad-spectrum antibiotics for tuberculosis, which informed antimicrobial stewardship practices.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that collaborative, community-led research platforms can overcome systemic barriers and enable more effective, locally driven responses to infectious disease threats, including mpox. This approach is novel in its emphasis on embedding research into routine healthcare delivery and fostering local evidence generation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that strengthening local research systems and fostering partnerships can improve the detection and response to emerging infectious diseases like mpox, leading to more equitable and effective public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single study and relies on reported outcomes from partnerships and webinars, which may lack direct, target-specific experimental data. However, the study provides a strong rationale for the importance of community-led research in addressing global health inequities.

喀麦隆共和国南部地区埃博拉瓦市传染病动态的SEIR+D流行病建模

SEIR+D epidemic modeling of infectious disease dynamics in Ebolowa, South Region, Republic of Cameroon
作者： Ramraj G., Poornima T.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估包括猴痘在内的传染病对埃博拉瓦市卫生系统的影响,并评估不同干预策略在减轻这些影响方面的有效性。该市有多种病原体反复暴发的历史,但其卫生系统容量仍然有限。
设计与对象
本研究采用SEIR+D流行病建模框架,模拟埃博拉瓦市的疾病动态,该市约有92,000居民,并作为约180,000人口的转诊中心。该模型通过2021–2022年的霍乱监测数据进行验证,代表了一种广泛可能的急性易爆发传染病。
核心方法
模型参数来源于世界卫生组织和国家数据,R0通过下一代矩阵方法得出。采用四阶龙格-库塔积分法,在180天的时间范围内,模拟三种预设的干预情景,求解常微分方程(ODE)系统。
主要结果
在没有干预的情况下,模型预测感染人数将在第119天达到峰值,为14,226人(占人口的15.5%)。攻击率将达到91.0%(约83,700例病例),导致2,608人死亡,高峰期床位需求达到200床位容量的356%。
解释与创新
作者认为,只有早期、强有力的干预措施才能与该市的卫生系统容量相匹配。他们强调,鉴于埃博拉瓦市有跨境暴发的历史,应优先考虑预先部署资源,而非在疫情信号出现后才采取的反应性措施。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,需要在埃博拉瓦市采取主动的公共卫生策略,特别是针对猴痘的暴发。它们强调了早期干预的重要性,以防止卫生系统超负荷运转。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于一个通用的SEIR+D模型,并通过霍乱数据进行校准,这可能无法完全代表猴痘的动态。证据强度中等,因为该模型并非专门针对猴痘,而是通过定性方式与相关数据进行验证。

SEIR+D epidemic modeling of infectious disease dynamics in Ebolowa, South Region, Republic of Cameroon

Authors: Ramraj G., Poornima T.
Original abstract (English)
Ebolowa, capital of Cameroon's South Region (≈ 92, 000 residents; referral hub for ≈ 180, 000), sits on the Cameroon–Gabon–Equatorial Guinea border and has recurrently faced cholera, malaria, Coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), and mpox, yet its universal health coverage (UHC) service-coverage index is only 44/100 and physician density is under 2 per 10,000 population. We develop a generic, disease-agnostic SEIR+D framework rather than one fitted to a single pathogen: the baseline parameter set (R 0 = 3.00) represents a broadly plausible acute epidemic-prone infection, checked qualitatively—not calibrated—against 2021–2022 cholera surveillance data. Parameters are drawn from the World Health Organization (WHO) and national sources; R 0 is derived via the next-generation matrix method; and the resulting ODE system is solved via fourth-order Runge–Kutta integration over an 180-day horizon under three pre-specified intervention scenarios. At baseline, without intervention, infections peak at 14,226 persons (15.5% of the population) on day 119. The attack rate reaches 91.0% (≈ 83, 700 cases), 2,608 people die, and peak bed demand reaches 356% of the 200-bed capacity. A moderate non-pharmaceutical intervention (NPI, 40% transmission reduction from day 30) cuts the attack rate to 26.8% but still reaches 98% bed capacity. A strong NPI (70% reduction from day 20) cuts deaths by 99.8% (to ≈ 4) and holds bed utilization below 1%. The ten-day timing advantage over moderate NPI alone saves roughly 538 additional lives. These results indicate that only early, aggressive intervention is compatible with Ebolowa's health-system capacity, and that, given the city's four-decade epidemic history, funded, pre-positioned–rather than reactive mobilization after a signal emerges—is warranted for this cross-border population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the potential impact of infectious diseases, including mpox, on Ebolowa's health system and to evaluate the effectiveness of different intervention strategies in mitigating these impacts. The city has a history of recurrent outbreaks of various pathogens, yet its health system capacity remains limited.
Design and population
The study employed a SEIR+D epidemic modeling framework to simulate disease dynamics in Ebolowa, a city with approximately 92,000 residents and a referral hub for a larger population of around 180,000. The model was checked against cholera surveillance data from 2021–2022, representing a broadly plausible acute epidemic-prone infection.
Core methods
The model parameters were sourced from the World Health Organization and national data, with R0 derived using the next-generation matrix method. The ordinary differential equation (ODE) system was solved using fourth-order Runge–Kutta integration over an 180-day period under three pre-specified intervention scenarios.
Main results
Without any intervention, the model predicts that infections would peak at 14,226 persons (15.5% of the population) on day 119. The attack rate would reach 91.0% (approximately 83,700 cases), resulting in 2,608 deaths and peak bed demand reaching 356% of the 200-bed capacity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that only early, aggressive interventions can align with the city's health system capacity. They highlight the importance of pre-positioned resources rather than reactive measures, given Ebolowa's history of cross-border outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the need for proactive public health strategies in Ebolowa, particularly for managing mpox outbreaks. They emphasize the importance of early intervention to prevent overwhelming the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a generic SEIR+D model calibrated against cholera data, which may not fully represent mpox dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as the model is not specifically tailored to mpox but is checked qualitatively against relevant data.

教育干预改善抗菌药物合理使用知识但揭示农村医院中持续存在的障碍

Educational Intervention Improves Antimicrobial Stewardship Knowledge but Reveals Persistent Barriers in Rural Hospitals
作者： Jack H. Lambert, Rayven Todd, Thomas W. Bagwell, Damani Andre, Raybun Spelts, Fantasia Gorham, Jamie Woods, Ayomide H. Adeyemi, Shondia Evans, Rafael Ponce‐Terashima
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:抗菌药物耐药性威胁全球公共卫生,而为农村社区提供服务的临界接入医院(CAHs)在实施抗菌药物管理计划(ASPs)方面面临结构性障碍。本研究评估了区域性教育干预是否能改善农村医疗专业人员的抗菌药物合理使用知识和实施情况。方法:在2023年和2024年的抗菌药物管理会议后,进行了一项定量、准实验的重复横断面评估。来自38家农村和临界接入医院的参与者完成了会前、会后、6个月随访和实施调查。定量数据使用描述性统计和非配对卡方检验进行分析,当预期单元格计数低于5时,使用Fisher精确检验,显著性水平设为p < 0.05。结果:两年间共有70名医疗专业人员参与。正确识别抗菌图谱用于指导经验性(而非确定性)治疗的比例从会前的27%提高到会后的58%(p = 0.029)。抗菌图谱解读的信心从会前的67%提升到会后的100%(p = 0.004,Fisher精确检验),但在6个月随访时下降至50%(10人中有5人)。关于构建抗菌图谱所需的最低分离株阈值的知识,从会前的23%(30人中有7人)提高到6个月随访时的64%(11人中有7人)(p = 0.026,Fisher精确检验)。尽管如此,对于通常不需要抗生素的疾病,处方意图的改善非常有限:中耳感染中抗生素使用的推荐比例从50%下降至36.84%,对于猴痘的推荐比例从20%下降至15.79%。所有参与者都表示有意修改临床实践;然而,时间限制、技术限制和资源不足被一致认为是实施过程中持续存在的主要障碍。结论:区域性教育干预改善了农村医疗专业人员在短期内的抗菌药物合理使用知识和信心,但知识保持随时间下降,结构性障碍依然存在。仅靠教育是不够的;需要持续强化以及结合受保护的ASPs时间、决策支持工具和外部合作的农村适应型ASPs模型,才能将知识提升转化为一致的抗菌药物管理实践。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗菌药物耐药性威胁全球公共卫生,而为农村社区提供服务的临界接入医院(CAHs)在实施抗菌药物管理计划(ASPs)方面面临结构性障碍。
设计与对象
本研究评估了区域性教育干预是否能改善农村医疗专业人员的抗菌药物合理使用知识和实施情况。来自38家农村和临界接入医院的参与者完成了会前、会后、6个月随访和实施调查。
核心方法
在2023年和2024年的抗菌药物管理会议后,进行了一项定量、准实验的重复横断面评估。定量数据使用描述性统计和非配对卡方检验进行分析,当预期单元格计数低于5时,使用Fisher精确检验,显著性水平设为p < 0.05。
主要结果
两年间共有70名医疗专业人员参与。正确识别抗菌图谱用于指导经验性(而非确定性)治疗的比例从会前的27%提高到会后的58%(p = 0.029)。抗菌图谱解读的信心从会前的67%提升到会后的100%(p = 0.004,Fisher精确检验),但在6个月随访时下降至50%(10人中有5人)。关于构建抗菌图谱所需的最低分离株阈值的知识,从会前的23%(30人中有7人)提高到6个月随访时的64%(11人中有7人)(p = 0.026,Fisher精确检验)。
解释与创新
区域性教育干预改善了农村医疗专业人员在短期内的抗菌药物合理使用知识和信心,但知识保持随时间下降,结构性障碍依然存在。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,尽管教育可以增强知识,但需要持续强化以及针对农村环境的抗菌药物管理模型,以应对持续存在的障碍并改善一致的抗菌药物管理实践。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告干预在6个月随访期之后的长期效果,也未提供结果在其他医疗环境中的可推广性信息。

Educational Intervention Improves Antimicrobial Stewardship Knowledge but Reveals Persistent Barriers in Rural Hospitals

Authors: Jack H. Lambert, Rayven Todd, Thomas W. Bagwell, Damani Andre, Raybun Spelts, Fantasia Gorham, Jamie Woods, Ayomide H. Adeyemi, Shondia Evans, Rafael Ponce‐Terashima
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Antimicrobial resistance threatens public health globally, and critical access hospitals (CAHs) serving rural communities face structural barriers to implementing antibiotic stewardship programs (ASPs). This study evaluated whether a regional educational intervention could improve antimicrobial stewardship knowledge and implementation among rural healthcare professionals. Methods: A quantitative, quasi-experimental repeated cross-sectional evaluation was conducted following Antibiotic Stewardship conferences in 2023 and 2024. Participants from 38 rural and critical access hospitals completed pre-conference, post-conference, 6-month follow-up, and implementation surveys. Quantitative data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and unpaired chi-square tests, with Fisher’s exact test substituted where expected cell counts were below 5, with significance set at p < 0.05. Results: Seventy healthcare professionals participated across both years. Correct identification of the use of an antibiogram to guide empiric (rather than definitive) therapy improved from 27% pre-conference to 58% post-conference (p = 0.029). Confidence in antibiogram interpretation increased from 67% to 100% post-intervention (p = 0.004, Fisher’s exact) but declined to 50% (5 of 10) at 6-month follow-up. Knowledge of the minimum isolate threshold required to construct an antibiogram improved from 23% (7 of 30) pre-conference to 64% (7 of 11) at 6-month follow-up (p = 0.026, Fisher’s exact). Despite this, prescribing intent for conditions where antibiotics are typically unnecessary showed minimal improvement: recommendations for antibiotic use in middle ear infections decreased from 50% to 36.84%, and for mpox from 20% to 15.79%. All participants reported intent to modify clinical practice; however, time constraints, technology limitations, and lack of resources were consistently cited as primary barriers for implementation across both survey years. Conclusions: A regional educational intervention improved short-term antimicrobial stewardship knowledge and confidence among rural healthcare professionals, but knowledge retention declined over time and structural barriers persisted. Education alone is insufficient; sustained reinforcement and rural-adapted ASP models incorporating protected ASP time, decision-support tools and external partnerships are needed to translate knowledge gains into consistent stewardship practice.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Antimicrobial resistance threatens public health globally, and critical access hospitals (CAHs) serving rural communities face structural barriers to implementing antibiotic stewardship programs (ASPs).
Design and population
This study evaluated whether a regional educational intervention could improve antimicrobial stewardship knowledge and implementation among rural healthcare professionals. Participants from 38 rural and critical access hospitals completed pre-conference, post-conference, 6-month follow-up, and implementation surveys.
Core methods
A quantitative, quasi-experimental repeated cross-sectional evaluation was conducted following Antibiotic Stewardship conferences in 2023 and 2024. Quantitative data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and unpaired chi-square tests, with Fisher’s exact test substituted where expected cell counts were below 5, with significance set at p < 0.05.
Main results
Seventy healthcare professionals participated across both years. Correct identification of the use of an antibiogram to guide empiric (rather than definitive) therapy improved from 27% pre-conference to 58% post-conference (p = 0.029). Confidence in antibiogram interpretation increased from 67% to 100% post-intervention (p = 0.004, Fisher’s exact) but declined to 50% (5 of 10) at 6-month follow-up. Knowledge of the minimum isolate threshold required to construct an antibiogram improved from 23% (7 of 30) pre-conference to 64% (7 of 11) at 6-month follow-up (p = 0.026, Fisher’s exact).
Interpretation and novelty
A regional educational intervention improved short-term antimicrobial stewardship knowledge and confidence among rural healthcare professionals, but knowledge retention declined over time and structural barriers persisted.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that while education can enhance knowledge, sustained reinforcement and tailored antibiotic stewardship models are necessary to address persistent barriers and improve consistent practice in rural settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the long-term effectiveness of the intervention beyond the 6-month follow-up period, nor does it provide information on the generalizability of the results to other healthcare settings.
